Analytic continuation of a PINN trained on the real momentum axis estimates the locations of poles and further reveals three complex-conjugate pole pairs. An independent reconstruction using PINN-parametrized spectral densities and a single pole pair yields $q=-0.146\pm0.391i~\mathrm{GeV}^2$. The agreement between the two methods substantially strengthens confidence in the properties of the leading singularities. Both spectral densities change sign, and the negative regions of the vector density and the complex poles indicate a violation of reflection positivity, consistent with quark confinement. Finally, we use the extracted poles and residues to calculate their contributions to two order parameters at finite quark chemical potential, the chiral condensate and the quark number density.

</details>


### [102] [Divergence-type hydrodynamics of radial oscillations in viscous neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33537)
*J. Peralta-Ramos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于牛顿极限下粘性中子星的简化模型，结合仅含体粘滞的二次耗散流体理论，研究了非线性效应对径向扰动演化的影响，并与线性流体力学结果进行对比。发现高体粘滞系数下，非线性理论表现出更强的阻尼、更长的非流体模式衰减时间以及更“刚性”的演化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非线性效应对高体粘滞中子星中长寿命非流体模式动力学的影响，以弥补线性理论的不足。

Method: 采用牛顿极限下的粘性中子星简化模型，耦合仅含体粘滞的二次耗散流体理论（发散型），分析径向扰动的非线性演化，并与线性流体力学结果对比。

Result: 在体粘滞系数 ζ_B ≳ 5×10³¹ g/cm/s 时，线性理论出现增长振荡，而非线性理论中该现象出现在更高 ζ_B 值；非线性理论表现出更强的振荡阻尼、更长的非流体模式衰减时间及更小的径向变形。

Conclusion: 非线性效应对于准确描述高体粘滞条件下中子星中长寿命非流体模式的动力学行为具有重要意义。

Abstract: Based on a simplified model of a viscous neutron star in the Newtonian limit coupled to a quadratic dissipative fluid theory of divergence type with bulk viscosity only, we explore the impact of nonlinear effects on the evolution of radial perturbations, and compare it with the results of linear hydrodynamics. For high values of the bulk viscosity $ζ_B \gtrsim 5\times 10^{31}$ g/cm/s, the linear theory develops growing oscillations, which do appear in the quadratic theory but at much higher values of $ζ_B$. The quadratic theory shows stronger damping of oscillations, longer decay rates of non-hydrodynamic modes and also a ``stiffer'' evolution of radial perturbations that results in less deformation. These results suggest that nonlinear effects may be relevant to capture the dynamics of the long-lived non-hydrodynamic modes present at high $ζ_B$.

</details>


### [103] [Determination of the strong coupling constant from the R ratio obtained in KEDR and BESIII experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33633)
*Korneliy Todyshev,Andrei Kataev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了KEDR和BESIII实验在低于粲夸克阈值能量下的数据与无质量QCD中e+e-湮灭R比的微扰展开式，研究了不同阶数截断对α_s(M_Z)提取值的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同阶数的微扰QCD近似对强耦合常数α_s(M_Z)提取结果的影响，以评估理论截断误差。

Method: 将KEDR和BESIII在低能区的e+e-湮灭R比实验数据，与无质量QCD中各阶微扰展开的R比理论表达式进行拟合。

Result: 拟合结果显示出所提取的α_s(M_Z)值明显依赖于微扰级数的截断阶数。

Conclusion: 微扰展开的截断阶数是影响α_s(M_Z)精度的关键系统性因素，需谨慎处理高阶修正以减小理论不确定性。

Abstract: The experimental data collected by the KEDR and BESIII collaborations at energies below the charm quark threshold are compared with the massless QCD expressions for the $e^+e^-$ annihilation R-ratio truncated at different orders of perturbation theory. The fits demonstrate the dependence of the extracted $α_s(M_Z)$ values on the orders of truncation of the corresponding approximations.

</details>


### [104] [Singly-strange hidden-bottom and double-bottom molecular pentaquarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33708)
*Fu-Lai Wang,Jia-Ning Cui,Rui Chen,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了单奇异隐底和双底分子型五夸克态的质量谱，基于单玻色子交换模型计算了Ξ_b^{(′,*)}B^{(*)}和Ξ_b^{(′,*)}B̄^{(*)}相互作用的有效势，并通过求解耦合道薛定谔方程寻找松散束缚态；预测了多个自旋-宇称-同位旋组合下的候选态，为LHCb等实验提供了明确搜寻目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受新发现的单奇异隐粲五夸克态P_{ψs}^Λ(4459)和P_{ψs}^Λ(4338)启发，探索底夸克区类似奇异分子型五夸克态的存在可能性。

Method: 采用单玻色子交换模型构建Ξ_b^{(′,*)}B^{(*)}系统的有效势；利用G-宇称规则推导Ξ_b^{(′,*)}B̄^{(*)}系统的势；求解含S-D波混合与耦合道效应的薛定谔方程以搜寻束缚态。

Result: 预言了多个Ξ_b^{(′,*)}B^{(*)}系统中具有特定I(J^P)量子数的松散束缚态作为单奇异隐底分子五夸克候选者；其对应的Ξ_b^{(′,*)}B̄^{(*)}体系则为单奇异双底分子五夸克候选者。

Conclusion: 该工作为实验在底夸克能区（如LHCb）搜寻新型奇异五夸克态提供了具体、系统的理论预言和候选态清单。

Abstract: Inspired by the observation of the singly-strange hidden-charm pentaquarks $P_{ψs}^Λ(4459)$ and $P_{ψs}^Λ(4338)$, we present a systematic investigation of the mass spectra of the singly-strange hidden-bottom and double-bottom molecular pentaquarks arising from the $Ξ_b^{(\prime,\,*)}B^{(*)}$ and $Ξ_b^{(\prime,\,*)}\bar B^{(*)}$ interactions. Using the one-boson-exchange model, we derive the effective potentials for the $Ξ_b^{(\prime,\,*)}B^{(*)}$ systems by exchanging light mesons. The effective potentials for the corresponding $Ξ_b^{(\prime,\,*)}\bar B^{(*)}$ systems are then obtained via the $G$-parity rule. By solving the coupled-channel Schrödinger equation with $S$-$D$ wave mixing and coupled-channel effects, we search for loosely bound states with all possible isospin and spin-parity quantum numbers. Our results show that the $Ξ_b B$ state with $I(J^P)=0(1/2^-)$, the $Ξ_b B^*$ states with $I(J^P)=0(1/2^-)$ and $0(3/2^-)$, the $Ξ_b^\prime B$ state with $I(J^P)=0(1/2^-)$, the $Ξ_b^* B$ state with $I(J^P)=0(3/2^-)$, the $Ξ_b^\prime B^*$ states with $I(J^P)=0(1/2^-)$ and $0(3/2^-)$, and the $Ξ_b^* B^*$ states with $I(J^P)=0(1/2^-)$, $0(3/2^-)$, and $0(5/2^-)$ are identified as promising candidates of the singly-strange hidden-bottom molecular pentaquarks. Their $Ξ_b^{(\prime,\,*)}\bar B^{(*)}$ partners are likewise predicted to be the singly-strange double-bottom molecular pentaquark candidates. These predictions provide concrete targets for future experimental searches for exotic hadrons in the bottom sector, for example at LHCb.

</details>


### [105] [Heavy quark drag coefficient using phonon-heavy quark EFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33709)
*Andreas Kirchner,Noah Koliadko,Berndt Mueller,Jyotirmoy Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于有效场论（EFT）方法，将重夸克与夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）中集体激发（声子）的相互作用建模，计算重夸克拖曳系数，并通过与AdS/CFT结果和高动量QCD结果匹配确定未知的威尔逊系数，最后与实验测得的扩散系数比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低动量重夸克在强耦合、高占据数的QGP介质中的第一性原理描述仍具挑战性，需发展替代性微扰描述框架。

Method: 构建并应用重夸克-声子有效场论（EFT），结合重夸克展开，计算拖曳系数；通过与AdS/CFT及高动量QCD结果匹配确定威尔逊系数。

Result: 得到了重夸克拖曳系数的EFT表达式，确定了关键的威尔逊耦合系数，并与AdS/CFT、QCD及实验扩散系数进行了定量比较。

Conclusion: EFT方法为理解低动量重夸克在QGP中的输运性质提供了系统、可扩展的理论框架，威尔逊系数的标定增强了该理论与第一性原理及实验的联系。

Abstract: The description of heavy quarks inside the QGP medium, especially at low momenta, remains challenging from first principles due to the strong coupling and large gluon occupation numbers. An alternative description in terms of perturbative degrees of freedom treats the interactions of heavy quarks with the collective excitations of the medium in the form of phonons. This description is derived as an effective field theory (EFT) and therefore allows for a naturally occurring power counting of the allowed interactions. This EFT has already been established in our previous work, where we derived the phonon-heavy quark interactions and calculated their thermal scattering cross section. In this work, we use these results, together with the heavy quark expansion, to calculate the heavy quark drag coefficient. Subsequently, we compare our results with the drag coefficient obtained from AdS/CFT calculations to determine the previously unknown Wilson coefficients for the quark-phonon coupling. Additionally, we determine the same Wilson coefficients by matching the EFT result for the drag force at low momenta with the QCD result at high momenta. Finally, we will compare our results with experimentally determined heavy quark diffusion coefficients.

</details>


### [106] [Higgsino dark matter explanation of LZ high-recoil event from deflected AMSB with $U(1)_X$ generated $μ$ term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33787)
*Ke-Lin Li,Fei Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在具有一个带电可见单态的超对称U(1)_X框架下研究希格斯微子暗物质，以解释LZ实验中的高反冲事例；通过六态中性微子混合矩阵分析质量劈裂，并结合偏离异常介质超对称破缺（deflected AMSB）模型讨论热历史、晚期信使衰变对暗物质丰度的影响，以及直接探测和太阳捕获等实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受LZ实验近期高反冲事例启发，探索希格斯微子作为暗物质候选者的可能性，并构建能自然产生合适质量劈裂与相互作用的超对称U(1)_X模型。

Method: 构建含U(1)_X规范对称性及一个带电单态的超对称模型；推导六态中性微子混合矩阵（含U(1)_X规微子与单微子）；分析不同deflected AMSB参数区域下的软质量层级；研究信使粒子晚期衰变对热历史与暗物质最终丰度的影响；结合直接探测与太阳捕获实验约束检验模型。

Result: 模型可自然实现500 GeV、1.1 TeV与100 TeV三类希格斯微子质量标度；希格斯微子不对称性|X_A|≈10⁻²可无调谐地解释248 keV反冲事例，其自旋相关散射截面接近当前实验灵敏度；晚期信使衰变可通过熵产稀释或补充暗物质丰度，且净稀释需满足信使主导且希格斯微子产生被充分抑制的条件。

Conclusion: 该U(1)_X超对称框架为希格斯微子暗物质提供了兼具理论自洽性与实验可检验性的新路径，尤其能自然解释LZ高反冲事例，并可通过未来直接探测与太阳中微子实验进一步验证。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent LZ high-recoil event, we propose to study the Higgsino dark matter in a supersymmetric $U(1)_X$ framework with one charged visible singlet. The gauge symmetry forbids the bare Higgs bilinear and the cubic $S^3$ while allowing the singlet interaction that generates an effective Higgsino mass after symmetry breaking. We present the six-state neutralino mixing matrix, including the $U(1)_X$ gaugino and the singlino, and the resulting neutral Higgsino mass splitting. Different deflected-anomaly-mediated parameter regimes provide the soft-mass hierarchies needed for light, 1.1 TeV and heavy Higgsino candidates. The cosmological consequences of the messenger sector in deflected AMSB can affect greatly the thermal history of the dark matter. Late messenger decays can supplement an underabundant thermal population or dilute an overabundant one through entropy production. These possibilities are governed by a common final-yield relation: a dominating messenger must have sufficiently suppressed Higgsino production for net dilution to succeed. We organise the 500 GeV, 1.1 TeV and 100 TeV reference regimes around this condition. Direct-detection and solar-capture constraints are discussed as tests of the resulting spectrum and final abundance. The elastic bino- or singlino-like interpretation of the same event is also realized in this spectrum: the Higgsino asymmetry $|X_A|\simeq10^{-2}$ that a $248\keV$ recoil requires arises without tuning, with a spin-dependent rate lying close to the present experimental sensitivity.

</details>


### [107] [Collisional dynamics of neutrino flavor conversion: angular structures, inhomogeneity, and decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33815)
*Masamichi Zaizen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了自相互作用中微子气体中的碰撞诱导味不稳定性，通过线性稳定性分析和非线性量子动力学模拟，揭示了能量依赖性、各向异性和空间非均匀性对味转换的影响，并发现近/无隙与远/有隙分支在波数依赖性、增长速率及本征结构上的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解致密中微子气体（如超新星爆发环境中）在自相互作用与额外非相干碰撞下的非线性味演化行为，特别是碰撞如何影响集体不稳定性与空间结构发展。

Method: 采用线性稳定性分析与非线性量子动力学模拟，系统考察能量依赖性、角分布各向异性及空间非均匀性对碰撞诱导中微子味不稳定性的影响。

Result: 发现近/无隙与远/有隙不稳定分支具有截然不同的波数依赖性与最大增长率位置；其本征矢展现出差异化的能量与角度结构，分别对应味交换与等分；非线性演化中出现二次味增长；碰撞退相干抑制空间不均匀性发展，使中微子分布趋于均匀。

Conclusion: 碰撞不仅改变不稳定性特征，还通过退相干限制空间结构增长；该工作为预测爆炸性天体环境中中微子非线性味演化提供了关键理论基础。

Abstract: Self-interacting neutrino gases exhibit collective behavior, in which additional incoherent collisions can induce collisional neutrino-flavor instability. We investigate the effects of energy dependence, anisotropy, and spatial inhomogeneity on the resulting flavor conversion through linear stability analysis and nonlinear quantum kinetic simulations. We find contrasting wavenumber dependences for the near/gapless and far/gapped branches, with maximum growth rates at collision-scale shifted wavenumbers and at collective-scale wavenumbers, respectively. Their eigenvectors reveal distinct energy and angular structures, which are reflected in the subsequent establishment of flavor swap and equipartition. The nonlinear evolution also features secondary flavor growth associated with changes in the stability of collective modes as the neutrino background evolves. Despite the spreading of flavor coherence across spatial scales due to nonlinear mode coupling, collisional decoherence tends to limit the development of spatial inhomogeneities, leaving the neutrino populations nearly homogeneous. These findings represent a step towards understanding and predicting the nonlinear flavor evolution of dense neutrino gases interacting with their surroundings, particularly in explosive astrophysical environments.

</details>


### [108] [Origin of flavour cancellations in thermal leptogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33862)
*Teruyuki Kitabayashi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了热轻子生成机制中重正中微子衰变产生的CP不对称性，发现对角与非对角部分之间的抵消主要由味依赖的洗出效应引起，且干涉项（特别是N2和N3）贡献显著；简化模型（仅用味分布近似CP不对称性）会严重低估该贡献，而采用完整密度矩阵方程则能更准确描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有三个层级重正中微子的热轻子生成机制中，重子不对称性可能源于大项间的精细抵消，但抵消发生的位置、干涉项的大小及其对常用近似方法精度的影响尚不明确。

Method: 将CP不对称性按带电轻子味基分解为对角与非对角部分，并用标准模型输运方程分别演化；对比不同近似方法（如仅用味分布 vs 密度矩阵方法）的预测结果。

Result: 抵消主要发生在同一重正中微子（如N1、N2或N3）的对角与非对角CP不对称性之间，由味依赖洗出导致；N2和N3的干涉项通常主导其总贡献；简化近似（忽略相干性）会丢失大部分干涉贡献，而完整密度矩阵方法与之吻合更好。

Conclusion: 准确计算热轻子生成需保留CP不对称性的完整矩阵结构（含干涉项），尤其对N2/N3主导情形；仅使用味分辨的标量近似会带来显著系统误差。

Abstract: In thermal leptogenesis with three hierarchical heavy neutrinos $N_1$, $N_2$ and $N_3$, the baryon asymmetry can be the small remainder of large contributions that cancel. Interference terms in the CP asymmetry are known to contribute to the final asymmetry through flavour-dependent washout. However, it remains unclear where the cancellation occurs, how large the interference contribution is, and how the accuracy of approximate equations changes when this information is lost. We split the CP asymmetry into diagonal and off-diagonal parts in the charged-lepton basis and evolve them with Standard Model transport equations. We find that the cancellation occurs mainly between the diagonal and off-diagonal parts of the same heavy neutrino, and that it is caused by flavour-dependent washout. We also find that the interference between the lepton state produced in the decay and the states orthogonal to it typically accounts for most of the $N_2$ and $N_3$ contribution. Approximations that reduce the CP asymmetry to flavour populations turn out to miss most of this contribution, whereas density-matrix equations with the full CP-asymmetry matrix agree much better.

</details>


### [109] [Revisiting the collisional energy loss of a heavy quark in an anisotropic quark-gluon plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33959)
*Mingda Cai,Yun Guo,Enke Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了在具有小动量空间各向异性的夸克-胶子等离子体中重夸克的碰撞能量损失，推导出硬贡献的解析表达式和软贡献的一维积分形式，并证明在各向异性的一阶展开下，人工分离尺度依赖性在两部分之间相互抵消；各向异性修正的角度依赖部分在三种不同归一化方案下均表现为与四极矩P2(cosα)成正比，其符号（增强或抑制能量损失）取决于夸克速度、耦合强度和传播角度的联合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重夸克在非平衡、各向异性夸克-胶子等离子体中的能量损失行为，弥补现有理论对小各向异性情形解析处理的不足，并澄清人工尺度依赖性和各向异性修正的普适结构。

Method: 基于Braaten-Thoma框架，分别计算硬散射贡献（获得闭式解）和软辐射贡献（约化为一维积分），并系统分析三种不同归一化方式下的各向异性分布函数（未归一化、固定粒子数密度、固定能量密度），比较其能量损失修正项的角度与参数依赖性。

Result: 1）硬软贡献间的人工分离尺度依赖性在各向异性线性阶精确抵消；2）各向异性修正的角度部分恒为P2(cosα)形式，与归一化方式无关；3）在固定能量密度条件下，仅保留四极矩项；4）修正符号由夸克速度、耦合强度和传播角共同决定，例如当P2系数为正时，在cos²α > 1/3方向增强能量损失，反之则抑制。

Conclusion: 重夸克在弱各向异性QGP中的碰撞能量损失具有普适的四极矩角度结构，其修正的大小和符号受动力学参数联合调控；该结果为利用重味探针诊断早期QGP各向异性的实验分析提供了可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: We revisit the collisional energy loss of a heavy quark in a quark-gluon plasma with a small momentum-space anisotropy. Within the Braaten-Thoma framework, we obtain the hard contribution in closed form and reduce the soft contribution to one-dimensional integrals. Our result demonstrates analytically that, at linear order in the anisotropy, the dependence on the artificial separation scale cancels between the two contributions. By considering the unnormalized anisotropic distribution together with two variants that hold either the parton number density or the energy density at its isotropic value, we find that the angle-dependent part of the anisotropy correction is identical in all three cases and is proportional to the quadrupole $P_2(\cosα)$, where $α$ is the angle between the heavy-quark velocity and the anisotropy direction. In addition, the sign of the anisotropy correction, which determines whether the energy loss is enhanced or suppressed relative to the isotropic result, depends jointly on the quark speed, coupling, and propagation angle. In particular, at fixed energy density, only the quadrupole term survives. For a positive coefficient of $P_2(\cosα)$, the correction enhances the energy loss for $\cos^2α>1/3$ and suppresses it for $\cos^2α<1/3$, with the opposite behavior for a negative coefficient.

</details>


### [110] [Coherent quantum-circuit simulation of Mueller dipole branching at small $x$: multiplicity fluctuations and DIS phenomenology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33979)
*Wei Kou,Xurong Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个用于小x区域Mueller偶极子分支过程的量子电路，通过泊松时钟均匀化和正交事件历史寄存器实现对分裂路径的相干编码；数值验证了其与马尔可夫模型的一致性，并通过经典模拟分析了连续横坐标下级联涨落特性及其与DIS实验现象（如H1实验中的多重数熵）的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在量子计算框架下精确建模强相互作用中小x区域的随机偶极子分支过程，并理解其涨落特性与深度非弹性散射（DIS）实验观测之间的关系。

Method: 构建受控有限态量子电路，采用泊松时钟均匀化控制更新次数，利用正交事件历史寄存器记录所有分裂路径；同时辅以经典连续横坐标下的Mueller级联模拟，计算高阶阶乘矩、多重数熵及偶极子尺寸区间的关联。

Result: 量子电路状态向量的多重数概率与截断时钟马尔可夫结果数值一致，与连续时间马尔可夫基准偏差约7×10⁻⁴；经典模拟显示最大快度下归一化四阶阶乘矩为1.81（Yule过程为2.84）；负二项响应模型能重现H1实验中电荷多重数熵随x减小而上升的趋势，但无法完全描述实测多重数分布。

Conclusion: 首次实现了小x Mueller偶极子分支过程的显式相干量子编码，明确了有限态量子模拟与连续级联涨落及DIS唯象学之间的定量联系。

Abstract: Mueller dipole branching at small $x$ is a stochastic process in which splitting rates depend on transverse geometry. We construct a coherent quantum circuit for a regulated finite-state version of this process. Poisson-clock uniformization controls the number of updates, while orthogonal event-history registers retain the splitting paths, including those leading to the same final configuration. The circuit prepares transition amplitudes from local splitting rates rather than loading a precomputed evolved distribution. Its statevector multiplicity probabilities reproduce the truncated-clock Markov result to numerical precision and agree with the full continuous-time Markov-chain benchmark within approximately $7\times10^{-4}$. To examine fluctuations beyond the small circuit, we independently simulate the Mueller cascade with continuous transverse coordinates. At the largest rapidity studied, its normalized fourth factorial moment is $1.81$, compared with $2.84$ for an equal-mean Yule process. We also determine multiplicity entropies and correlations between dipole-size sectors. Applying a fixed negative-binomial dipole-to-charged-particle response reproduces the rising trend of the H1 charged-particle multiplicity entropy toward smaller $x$, but does not fully describe the measured multiplicity distributions. These results demonstrate an explicit coherent encoding of finite-state dipole branching and clarify its relation to continuous-cascade fluctuations and DIS phenomenology.

</details>


### [111] [Determining the kaon distribution amplitudes by fitting the $D\to K$ transition form factor to investigate the equivalence of two chiral LCSR frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34007)
*Fang-Ping Peng,Sheng-Bo Wu,Hai-Bing Fu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛方法拟合BESIII实验数据，结合QCD轻锥求和规则（LCSRs）计算D→K矢量跃迁形状因子（TFF），并利用不同模型描述kaon分布振幅，进而提取CKM矩阵元|Vcs|及重要比值R，结果与实验和其他理论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定D→K跃迁形状因子对检验标准模型、特别是提取CKM矩阵元|Vcs|至关重要；现有实验数据（如BESIII）亟需与理论方法（如LCSRs）结合以提升精度和模型依赖性理解。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛（MC）方法拟合BESIII实验数据得到TFF；同时用QCD轻锥求和规则分别基于左右手手征流计算TFF：右手流依赖kaon扭度-2分布振幅（采用R1/R2/R3三种模型并拟合参数），左手流依赖扭度-3分布振幅（L4，基于LCHO框架并由理论约束定参）。

Result: 在大反冲区得到f^{DK}_+(0)的四组结果（R1–R3与L4），误差范围明确；由此计算D→Keν_e衰变宽度、分支比及|V_{cs}|，并预测比值R_{π^-/K^-}^e和R_{π^0/K^0}^e；所有结果与实验和其他理论一致。

Conclusion: 不同kaon分布振幅模型对TFF影响较小，LCSRs结合实验拟合是可靠方法；所得|V_{cs}|及衰变比值支持标准模型，为后续高精度味物理研究提供基准。

Abstract: In this work, we fit the $D\to K$ vector transition form factor (TFF) to the BESIII experimental data using a Monte Carlo (MC) method. We also calculate the TFF using QCD light-cone sum rules (LCSRs) with left-and right-handed chiral currents. For the TFF derived using the right-handed chiral current, the key input is the kaon twist-2 distribution amplitude (DA), for which we consider three models: the power-law parametrization ($\mathrm{R1}$), the truncated Gegenbauer expansion ($\mathrm{R2}$), and the light-cone harmonic oscillator (LCHO) model ($\mathrm{R3}$). The parameters of these models are determined by fitting the corresponding TFF predictions to the MC-fit results. For the TFF derived using the left-handed chiral current, the dominant input is the kaon twist-3 DA ($\mathrm{L4}$), which is constructed within the LCHO framework. Its parameters are determined from theoretical constraints rather than from the fit. In the large-recoil region, the resulting TFF are $f^{DK}_+(0)|_{\rm{R1}}=0.7381^{+0.0180}_{-0.0194}$, $f^{DK}_+(0)|_{\rm{R2}}=0.7320^{+0.0178}_{-0.0192}$, $f^{DK}_+(0)|_{\rm{R3}}=0.7324^{+0.0180}_{-0.0191}$, and $f^{DK}_+(0)|_{\rm{L4}}=0.7343^{+0.0459}_{-0.0573}$. We then calculate the decay widths and branching fractions of the semileptonic decays $D\to K eν_e$ and extract the CKM matrix element $|V_{cs}|$. Finally, we predict the two important ratios, $\mathcal{R}_{π^-/K^-}^{e}$ and $\mathcal{R}_{π^0/K^0}^{e}$. All the obtained results are consistent with existing experimental measurements and theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [112] [Non-perturbative charm production from color ropes in relativistic heavy ion collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34009)
*Carl B. Rosenkvist,Marcus Bleicher*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用扩展的SMASH输运模型研究了核-核反应中的粲夸克产生，发现弦融合可能提供显著的非微扰粲夸克产生源，有助于解释低横动量区观测到的粲夸克增强现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释实验中观察到的低横动量区粲夸克产额高于微扰QCD预期的‘粲夸克增强’现象。

Method: 采用扩展的SMASH输运模型，引入色绳（color rope）形成机制，模拟√s_NN = 5–20 GeV能区的核-核碰撞中的粲夸克产生过程。

Result: 弦融合可提供显著的额外非微扰粲夸克产额；预测的中性D介子产额与当前实验约束一致。

Conclusion: 弦融合机制可能是低能区粲夸克增强的重要物理来源，该结果对NA61/SHINE等实验具有指导意义。

Abstract: We present results on charm production in nucleus-nucleus reactions in the energy range $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=5$-$20$ GeV, obtained with the SMASH transport approach extended to include color rope formation. We show that string fusion may provide a substantial additional non-perturbative source of charm production. This may help to explain the apparent charm enhancement (above the pQCD expectations) at low transverse momenta. Our predicted neutral $D$-meson yields are consistent with current experimental constraints and may provide valuable insights for upcoming data by the NA61/SHINE collaboration.

</details>


### [113] [Towards Quark-Gluon Correlations in Pion from the Dyson-Schwinger Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34030)
*Zanbin Xing,Jiayin Kang,Lei Chang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了π介子的chiral-odd twist-3分布函数e(x)，指出先前彩虹阶梯（RL）计算中忽略Wilson线导致违反运动方程（EOM）求和规则；作者证明Wilson线在耦合一级贡献三类平面图，逐类抵消EOM违规，从而精确恢复求和规则，并给出完整的零阶与一阶矩结果；Wilson线显著增强e(x)负区幅度并改变符号位置，是RL框架下描述e(x)所缺失的关键成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决先前彩虹阶梯（RL）方法计算π介子chiral-odd twist-3分布e(x)时因忽略Wilson线而导致的第一矩EOM求和规则被违反的问题。

Method: 在QCD框架下，分析Wilson线在耦合常数一级对e(x)的贡献，识别并计算其产生的三类平面费曼图，验证其逐类抵消EOM违规的效果，并结合RL近似得到e(x)的零阶与一阶矩。

Result: Wilson线在一级耦合下三类图精确恢复EOM求和规则；获得完整的RL零阶与一阶矩；e(x)的真正twist-3分量在x≈0.45处变号，且Wilson线使其负区幅度增加一倍以上。

Conclusion: Wilson线是RL方法中描述e(x)所缺失的关键物理成分；该工作为后续全阶gA展开重求和奠定基础。

Abstract: Twist-3 distributions encode quark-gluon correlations, and the pion's chiral-odd $e(x)$ is the simplest of them. QCD fixes its lowest moments exactly, yet the only rainbow-ladder (RL) calculation of $e(x)$ omitted the Wilson line and violated the equation-of-motion (EOM) sum rule for the first moment. We show that the discrepancy is not a failure of RL but of the omitted link: at first order in the coupling, the link contributes three classes of planar diagrams that cancel the EOM violation class by class, so the sum rule is restored exactly in RL. The resulting zeroth and first moments are complete RL results. The genuine twist-3 component changes sign near $x=0.45$, and the Wilson line more than doubles its magnitude in the negative region. This identifies the Wilson line as the missing ingredient in the RL description of $e(x)$ and sets the stage for a full resummation of the $gA$ expansion.

</details>


### [114] [Black hole accretion flows as dark matter bolometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34172)
*Ran Ding,Ningqiang Song,Cong Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用超大质量黑洞周围的吸积流作为轻暗物质的量热器，通过分析暗物质与等离子体电子的弹性散射对电子冷却效应及其辐射谱的影响，结合事件视界望远镜对M87*的观测数据，首次利用热能平衡限制keV–MeV质量范围暗物质与电子的相互作用强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体物理系统可有效补充直接探测实验对暗物质相互作用的研究，尤其是对亚MeV质量暗物质的探测存在空白，需发展新探针。

Method: 将暗物质冷却效应纳入M87*周围的径移主导吸积流（ADAF）模型，求解包含暗物质能量交换的完整热力学方程组，计算电子温度剖面及射电至毫米波段辐射谱，并与EHT观测谱对比。

Result: 基于M87*吸积流热平衡，首次给出keV至MeV质量暗物质与电子弹性散射的严格限制，涵盖重、轻传播子情形。

Conclusion: 黑洞吸积流可作为高灵敏度的天然暗物质探测平台，该方法为轻质量暗物质参数空间提供了新的强约束，拓展了多信使天体物理暗物质研究范式。

Abstract: Astrophysical systems provide powerful probes of dark matter interactions, complementing direct-detection searches. We propose using accretion flows of the supermassive black hole as a bolometer for light DM. The kinetic energy of sub-MeV dark matter in the black hole gravitational potential can remain below the thermal energy of the plasma electrons. The colder dark matter population can therefore absorb energy through elastic scattering, cooling the electrons and modifying their emission spectrum. We integrate dark matter cooling into the advection-dominated accretion flow around M87$^{*}$ and solve the full set of thermodynamic equations to obtain the resulting temperature profile and radio-to-millimeter emission. By comparing the predicted spectrum with observations accompanying the Event Horizon Telescope campaign, we use, for the first time, the thermal energy balance of the hot accretion flow to constrain DM-electron scattering, obtaining stringent limits on keV-to-MeV DM for both heavy and light mediators.

</details>


### [115] [Lower Bound on the Lightest Neutrino Mass with Resonant Neutrino Self-Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34213)
*Shao-Feng Ge,Joao Paulo Pinheiro,Shaoyang Qin,Ruitao Sun,Oleg Titov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用超高能天体物理中微子与低能宇宙中微子背景之间的s道共振散射，来对最轻中微子质量设定下限的可能性，并通过引入约1 MeV质量的轻标量媒介子实现该机制；作者从第一性原理出发推导了包含双重再生项的中微子与反中微子联合输运方程，并将模拟事例谱与IceCube 7.5年HESE数据对比，从而给出明确的质量下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为确定最轻中微子质量的下限，利用超高能天体物理中微子与宇宙中微子背景的共振散射效应。

Method: 引入O(1) MeV质量的轻标量媒介子实现中微子自相互作用，分析s道共振的运动学与截面特性，从第一性原理推导含双重再生项的中微子与反中微子双输运方程。

Result: 将模拟事件谱与IceCube 7.5年HESE数据对比，得到最轻中微子质量的明确下限。

Conclusion: s道共振散射可用于约束最轻中微子质量，该方法具有可观测性和理论自洽性。

Abstract: We show the possibility of using the resonant $s$-channel scattering of ultra high energy astrophysical neutrinos with the low energy cosmic neutrino background to put a lower bound on the lightest neutrino mass. This can be realized and illustrated by neutrino self-interactions with a light scalar mediator of $\mathcal O(1)$ MeV mass. After analyzing the kinematics and cross section features of the $s$-channel resonance, we derive from first principles the double transport equations with double regeneration terms for the coupled evolution of neutrinos and anti-neutrinos. The simulated event spectrum is then compared with the HESE 7.5 year data observed at IceCube to give a clear lower bound on the lightest neutrino mass.

</details>


### [116] [Complete NNLO QCD Calculations of $B_c^{(*)}\to J/ψ(η_c)$ Form Factors and Semileptonic Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34263)
*Wei Tao,Zhen-Jun Xiao,Ruilin Zhu,Qin Chang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次完成了B_c^{(*)}→J/ψ(η_c)跃迁中34个矢量、轴矢量、张量和轴张量形状因子的NNLO QCD计算，并结合z-级数拟合与NRQCD方法，预测了衰变宽度、分支比及轻子普适性比值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高B_c介子弱衰变过程理论预言精度，解决固定阶NRQCD在类时区域的阈值不稳定性问题，并为实验测量（如LHCb）提供高精度理论基准。

Method: 基于NRQCD因子化框架，采用FeynCalc和AMFlow实现自动化多圈计算；在类空q²区域和q²≈0处获得高精度（40位有效数字）结果；通过z-级数外推至全物理区域；结合LO/NLO/NNLO修正预测衰变观测量。

Result: NNLO修正使NLO结果提升约20%，显著减小重整化能标依赖；z-级数外推结果与最新格点QCD对B_c→J/ψ的计算一致；NNLO中心值R(B_c→J/ψ)=0.2836，介于格点QCD与LHCb最新测量之间。

Conclusion: 该工作为B_c介子半轻衰变提供了目前最精确的理论预言，验证了NRQCD+z-级数方法的有效性，且LFU比值的理论不确定性主要源于拟合点选取，而非高阶QCD修正。

Abstract: We present the first complete next-to-next-to-leading-order (NNLO) QCD calculation of all 34 vector, axial-vector, tensor, and axial-tensor form factors for the four $B_c^{(*)}\to J/ψ(η_c)$ transitions within nonrelativistic QCD (NRQCD) factorization. Using automated multi-loop techniques based on \texttt{FeynCalc} and \texttt{AMFlow}, we provide a dataset with at least 40 significant digits at 35 exact rational points in the $(m_b,m_c,q^2)$ parameter space, covering $q^2\in[-10,10]~\mathrm{GeV}^2$. In the spacelike region ($q^2<0$) and near $q^2=0$, the NLO corrections typically enhance the LO form factors by $\mathcal{O}(70\%)$, while the NNLO corrections amount to $\mathcal{O}(20\%)$ of the NLO predictions and substantially reduce the renormalization-scale dependence, demonstrating good perturbative convergence. In contrast, fixed-order NRQCD predictions suffer from threshold instabilities and become less reliable at higher timelike $q^2$. We therefore extrapolate the form factors to the full physical region via $z$-series fits to the NRQCD results at spacelike and near-zero $q^2$, finding broad agreement with the latest lattice QCD results for $B_c\to J/ψ$. With the resulting NRQCD+$z$-series form factors, we predict the decay widths, branching fractions, and lepton-flavor-universality (LFU) ratios for $B_c^{(*)}\to J/ψ(η_c)\,\ellν_\ell$ ($\ell=e,μ,τ$) at LO, NLO, and NNLO. Higher-order corrections substantially enhance the absolute rates but largely cancel in the ratios, whose uncertainties are dominated by the choice of fitting points. In particular, our NNLO central prediction $\mathcal{R}(B_c\to J/ψ)=0.2836$ lies between the latest lattice QCD determination and the most recent LHCb measurement.

</details>


### [117] [Isospin violating decay of D wave charmonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34394)
*Xing-Dao Guo,Qi Wu,Dian-Yong Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了ψ(3770)、ψ(3823)和ψ(3842)等D波粲偶素的同位旋破缺衰变过程，特别是到J/ψπ和h_cπ的衰变，并基于ψ(3770)→J/ψπ实验数据拟合模型参数α，进而预测了相关分支比和衰变宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于近期实验上清晰观测到同位旋破缺过程ψ(3770)→J/ψπ，作者希望拓展研究同类D波粲偶素ψ(3823)和ψ(3842)的同位旋破缺衰变行为，并给出可检验的理论预言。

Method: 利用ψ(3770)→J/ψπ的实验分支比拟合模型参数α，再将该参数应用于计算ψ(3770)→h_cπ、ψ(3823)→J/ψπ/h_cπ以及ψ(3842)→J/ψπ/h_cπ等同位旋破缺衰变过程的衰变宽度与分支比。

Result: 得到ψ(3770)→h_cπ分支比为(2.7~4.4)×10⁻⁷；ψ(3823)→J/ψπ和h_cπ的偏衰变宽度分别为1.12~1.77 eV和0.71~1.04 eV；ψ(3842)→J/ψπ和h_cπ的偏衰变宽度分别为0.028~0.044 eV和0.34~0.49 eV。

Conclusion: 这些理论预言为BES III、Belle II和LHCb等实验提供了明确的检验目标，有助于深入理解D波粲偶素的同位旋破缺机制及强子物理中的非微扰效应。

Abstract: The experimental observations of $ψ(3823)$ and $ψ(3842)$ enrich the $D$ wave charmonia family. In our previous work the decays of $ψ(3823)$ and $ψ(3842)$ to isospin conserved non-$D\bar D$ hadronic decays are well studied. Since the isospin violated decay channel $ψ(3770)\to J/ψπ$ is measured clearly very recently, so in this work we fit the model parameter $α$ through $BR(ψ(3770)\to J/ψπ)_{exp}$, then study the contribution of isospin violated decay channels of $ψ(3770)\to h_cπ$, $ψ(3823)$ and $ψ(3842)$ to $J/ψπ$ and $h_cπ$, respectively. With this range of parameters space, the branching ratio of $ψ(3770)\to h_cπ$ is estimated to be $(2.7\sim4.4)\times10^{-7}$. The decay widths of $ψ(3823)$ and $ψ(3842)$ to $J/ψπ$ and $h_cπ$ are also estimated. Our results show that the partial width of $ψ(3823)\to J/ψπ$ is $1.12\sim1.77$ eV, the value for $ψ(3823)\to h_cπ$ is $0.71\sim1.04$ eV, and $0.028\sim0.044$ eV for $ψ(3842)\to J/ψπ$, $0.34\sim0.49$ eV for $ψ(3842)\to h_cπ$, respectively. We hope those values could be tested by further precise measurements from the BES III, Belle II and LHCb Collaborations.

</details>


### [118] [Lighting up LZ with Loopy Transitions in a leptophilic Dark Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34438)
*Simone Biondini,Clara Hofheinz,Stefan Melchin,Lorenzo Tiberi,Stefan Vogl*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个通过轻子亲和t通道门户连接的标准模型的多组分暗物质部门，特别关注LUX-ZEPLIN实验观测到的高能核反冲事件，提出近简并暗物质部门中的吸热非弹性散射可解释该事件，并满足暗物质丰度约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验最近报道的高能核反冲事件启发，探索多组分暗物质模型中吸热非弹性散射对高能信号的解释能力。

Method: 构建含带电类矢量费米子中介的轻子亲和t通道暗物质模型，分析标量与矢量暗物质候选者通过同一中介耦合至标准模型轻子；计算非弹性与弹性核散射的有效算符维度，评估其对氙核反冲谱的影响，并结合暗物质热产额约束确定兼容参数空间。

Result: 发现吸热非弹性散射在有效算符维度上（五维）优于弹性散射（六维），自然增强高能信号；模型可在满足暗物质丰度前提下解释LZ观测到的单个248±46 keV核反冲事件。

Conclusion: 该轻子亲和多组分暗物质模型为LZ异常事件提供了一种自洽且自然的理论解释，凸显非弹性散射在直接探测中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study the direct detection phenomenology and cosmology of a multicomponent dark sector with a leptophilic t-channel portal. The coupling to right-handed Standard Model leptons is mediated by a charged vector-like fermion, allowing both a scalar and a vector dark candidate to connect to the SM through the same mediator. Motivated by the high-energy nuclear-recoil event recently reported by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment, we consider a nearly degenerate dark sector with mass splittings of order a few hundred keV and investigate the resulting inelastic transitions. Concretely, we focus on endothermic scattering of the dark states, which can shift the recoil spectrum towards the high-energy region preferred by LZ while suppressing low-energy events. We scrutinize the effective operators generating the dark-matter-photon coupling, which provides the essential interaction for nuclear recoils. Interestingly, the inelastic transition arises at dimension five whereas elastic scattering on nuclei is induced only at dimension six. This naturally explains the prominence of endothermic scattering compared to its elastic counterpart. We determine the parameter space compatible with the observed dark matter abundance and scrutinize the event rate and signal shape for scattering on xenon. Our results demonstrate that the observation of a single $E_R=248\pm 46$ keV nuclear recoil event in LZ can be accommodated by thermally produced dark matter in a leptophilic dark sector.

</details>


### [119] [Inverted Pendulum in the Sky: Floquet Stabilization of Cosmological Axions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34439)
*Tokiro Numasawa,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了通过快速振荡耦合（类似Kapitza摆）动态稳定轴子势能顶点（hilltop）的可能性，利用Floquet理论分析了多种实现方式的稳定性窗口，并指出这些机制虽不能普遍消除初值精细调节问题，但可延迟场开始振荡的时间并增强轴子暗物质丰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多宇宙学模型要求标量场位于其势能顶部，这通常需要精细调节的初始条件；本文旨在探究能否通过动力学机制（如快速振荡耦合）实现该顶点的自动稳定，从而缓解精细调节问题。

Method: 采用Floquet理论分析hilltop的稳定性窗口，并研究三种实现方式：振荡耦合、旋转或振荡的第二标量场、以及基于压缩三维球面sigma模型的陀螺类类比（倒立旋转陀螺）。同时考虑膨胀宇宙背景下的演化行为。

Result: 在膨胀宇宙中，稳定相持续e-fold数为尺度层级对数量级，与宇宙背景无关；但所研究的简单实现均无法同时满足吸引场至顶点并由哈勃摩擦将其冻结；在辐射或物质主导时期无法兼具吸引与冻结，在暴胀时期则受哈勃摩擦和能量预算限制；因此不能普遍消除精细调节，但可延迟振荡起始并提升轴子丰度比例 n_φ/n_{φ,std} ∝ (m̃/H_c)^{3/2}。

Conclusion: 所研究的动力学稳定机制虽不能从根本上解决hilltop初值精细调节问题，但具有实际应用价值——例如延迟轴子振荡、增强其暗物质丰度，并对暴胀、精质场（quintessence）及曲率子（curvatons）等模型具启示意义。

Abstract: Many cosmological scenarios require a scalar field to sit near the top of its potential, which usually requires a fine-tuned initial condition. We ask whether the hilltop of an axion potential can be dynamically stabilized by a rapidly oscillating coupling, as for the inverted pendulum of Kapitza, and to what extent this can reduce the fine-tuning. Using Floquet theory, we determine the stability window of the hilltop and analyze several realizations: an oscillating coupling, a spinning or oscillating second field, and a gyroscopic analogue, an inverted spinning top realized by a sigma model on a squashed three-sphere. In an expanding Universe the stabilized phase lasts for a number of $e$-folds logarithmic in the hierarchy of scales, independently of the cosmological background. Classifying the stabilizers by how their effective frequency redshifts, we show that in these realizations the hilltop cannot both attract the field and leave it frozen by the Hubble friction during radiation or matter domination, and that during inflation the attraction is limited by the Hubble friction and the energy budget. Thus the simple realizations studied here do not generically remove the hilltop fine-tuning. They can instead delay the onset of the oscillation and enhance the relic abundance as $n_φ/n_{φ,{\rm std}}\propto(\tilde m/H_c)^{3/2}$ in radiation domination. We discuss the implications for axion dark matter, inflation, quintessence and curvatons.

</details>


### [120] [Ultralight Dark Matter Spectrum with Gravitational Focusing and Doppler Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34464)
*Shao-Feng Ge,Yong Tang,Yue-Hui Yao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超轻暗物质在实验中产生的信号谱如何受到太阳引力聚焦和探测器运动引起的多普勒效应的影响，并通过解析估计和贝叶斯分析模拟数据，展示了这些局部环境效应对暗物质参数推断的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻暗物质的信号谱由其相空间分布决定，而局部环境效应（如太阳引力聚焦和探测器运动）会改变观测谱，若忽略会导致参数估计偏差。

Method: 通过解析方法估计信号显著性，并对模拟数据进行贝叶斯分析。

Result: 太阳引力聚焦和多普勒效应显著修改信号谱，影响暗物质参数的准确推断。

Conclusion: 在建模超轻暗物质信号时，必须包含局部环境效应，以避免参数估计偏差。

Abstract: Ultralight dark matter can produce signals in experiments through its couplings with the Standard Model particles. The resulting signal spectrum is determined by the phase-space distribution of dark matter, which encodes information about its production mechanism and the history of the local universe. However, the local environmental effects can modify the observed spectrum and should be included in signal modeling to avoid biased parameter estimates. In this work, we investigate how the gravitational focusing by Sun and the Doppler effect due to the detector's motion modify the signal spectrum and affect inferring the underlying dark matter parameters. We analytically estimate the signal significance and perform Bayesian analysis on simulated data to demonstrate their impact.

</details>


### [121] [Conditional multiparameter thermometry with neutrino oscillations: MSW energy diversity and a plasma-noise benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34475)
*Jilali Loulijat,Abdallah Slaoui,El Hassan Saidi,Mohamed Gouighri,Berihu Teklu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定平均电子密度的双味中微子退相干模型中，如何通过量子 Fisher 信息矩阵推断温度参数；发现单能测量下信息矩阵秩至多为1，需多能量分辨样本实现局域可识别性；物质依赖混合与双探针方案有助于区分温度效应与噪声；给出了Debye-cell模型下的量子信噪比估计，并强调完全相位平均会消除纵向相干衰减所携带的温度信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在太阳中微子物理中，准确推断温度参数对理解恒星内部结构和核反应过程至关重要；然而，在受限的两味中微子退相干模型中，温度参数的可识别性面临理论挑战，尤其当退相干效应主导时。

Method: 采用量子 Fisher 信息矩阵（QFIM）分析参数可识别性；构建双区域温度模型并引入能量分辨采样；利用物质依赖混合和双探针Bell测量方案评估区分能力；结合Debye-cell近似估算条件纵向量子信噪比；分析完全相位平均对温度信息的影响。

Result: 单能情况下QFIM秩≤1，无法局域识别两个温度；能量分辨采样且热敏感矢量非共线时恢复可识别性；物质混合提供不同权重；双探针可区分温度与空间噪声相关性；Debye-cell估计给出约3.1×10⁻¹³的信噪比（10 MeV，1太阳半径）；完全相位平均抹去仅由纵向相干衰减携带的温度信息。

Conclusion: 温度参数的条件态可识别性不等价于实际可观测的温度推断；保留太阳温度敏感性需额外考虑粒子数、折射或产生效应，不能仅依赖退相干动力学；该工作厘清了量子计量理论与太阳中微子实验解释之间的关键界限。

Abstract: We address temperature-parameter inference in a restricted two-flavor neutrino dephasing model with fixed mean electron densities. When two zone temperatures enter the propagated state via a single accumulated dephasing exponent, the single-energy quantum Fisher information matrix has rank at most 1. Independent, energy-resolved samples restore regular local identifiability when their thermal-sensitivity vectors are noncollinear. Matter-dependent mixing provides distinct weights in an illustrative two-zone model. A separate two-probe benchmark shows how complementary Bell sectors can distinguish temperature from spatial noise correlations. An electron-only Debye-cell estimate gives a conditional longitudinal quantum signal-to-noise ratio of approximately $3.1\times10^{-13}$ at 10 MeV for a uniform central-density medium over one solar radius. This estimate is not a microscopic upper bound or a detector sensitivity forecast. Complete phase averaging removes temperature information carried solely by longitudinal coherence damping; to retain solar sensitivity, a separate calculation of population, refractive, or production effects is required. The results distinguish conditional state identifiability from operational temperature inference.

</details>


### [122] [Recent Developments in Colibri: A Neural-Network PDF Model and Improved Bayesian Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34502)
*Ella Cole,Valentina Schütze Sánchez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Colibri开源框架的最新进展，包括colibri-n3fit的发布和高效贝叶斯采样器的集成，以支持神经网络PDF拟合与贝叶斯分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持PDF模型架构与不确定性传播方法的系统性比较，需一个灵活、开放且兼容多种推断方法的框架。

Method: 开发并集成了colibri-n3fit（n3fit神经网络模型的开源实现）与升级的高效贝叶斯采样器到Colibri框架中。

Result: 使Colibri具备执行神经网络PDF拟合的能力，并首次实现对n3fit架构的直接定量贝叶斯分析。

Conclusion: 更新后的Colibri显著拓展了其在PDF拟合中的适用性与灵活性，为模型对比研究提供了新工具与途径。

Abstract: We present recent developments in Colibri, an open-source framework for flexible fits of parton distribution functions (PDFs). Colibri allows the implementation of any PDF functional form as well as Bayesian, Monte Carlo, and Hessian inference methods, making it particularly well suited for systematic comparisons of model architectures and uncertainty propagation treatments. In these proceedings, we report on two key updates to the framework: the release of colibri-n3fit, an open-source implementation of the neural-network model used by the NNPDF collaboration, and the integration of an upgraded, highly efficient Bayesian sampler, bridging the gap between the computational cost of Bayesian PDF fitting and other uncertainty propagation treatments. Combined, these updates extend Colibri's capabilities to neural-network PDF fits, enabling direct, quantitative Bayesian analyses of the n3fit architecture and opening new avenues for comparative studies.

</details>


### [123] [Majorana Neutrino Exchange in Free-Baryon $B_1B_2\to ppe^-e^-$ Reactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34504)
*Yuan-Guo Xu,Bo Lan,Ben-Hou Xiang,Shuang-Shi Fang,Yi-Bing Zhang,Ru-Min Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了六种非奇异和奇异初态的自由重子反应 $B_1B_2\to pp e^-e^-$，统一处理轻/重马约拉纳中微子交换机制，给出了不同参数下的截面计算结果与谱特征，拓展了无中微子双贝塔衰变的自由重子类比研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展无中微子双贝塔衰变（0νββ）的自由重子类比过程至含奇异夸克的初态，并在统一框架下对比轻、重马约拉纳中微子交换的可观测效应，为未来实验提供理论基准。

Method: 在重子层次有效理论框架下，计算六种初态（含非奇异与奇异重子对）的反应截面；分别处理轻中微子（质量项 $m_{ββ}$ 主导）和重中微子（混合参数 $η_N=|U_{eN}|^2/m_N$ 主导）交换机制；考虑离壳截断 $Δq^2$ 对相空间及阈值行为的影响；分析不变质量谱特征与CKM动力学排序。

Result: 轻中微子交换截面正比于 $m_{ββ}^2$，在 $\sqrt{s}=5.1~\mathrm{GeV}$、$m_{ββ}=30~\mathrm{meV}$、$Δq^2=10^{-2}~\mathrm{GeV}^2$ 时介于 $9.28×10^{-49}$–$1.78×10^{-45}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$；重中微子交换截面正比于 $η_N^2$，相同能量下介于 $7.55×10^{-45}$–$1.98×10^{-41}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$（取 $η_N=4.0×10^{-9}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$）；重中微子谱峰出现在更小的质子对和更大的电子对不变质量平方处；两种机制均呈现CKM驱动的通道顺序（$nn$ 截面最大）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将自由重子0νββ类比推广至奇异初态，定量揭示了轻/重中微子交换在截面大小、能谱形状及相空间依赖上的系统性差异，为高能强子对撞机上探测马约拉纳中微子性质提供了关键理论支撑。

Abstract: The free-baryon reactions $B_1B_2\to pp e^-e^-$ are studied for six nonstrange and strange initial states, with light- and heavy-Majorana-neutrino exchange treated in the same baryon-level framework. For light-neutrino exchange, the cross sections scale with the square of the effective Majorana mass $m_{ββ}$. The calculated cross sections increase as the off-shell cutoff $Δq^2$ is reduced, especially near threshold and in the hyperon channels, because more of the phase-space region where the squared four-momentum of the exchanged neutrino is close to zero is retained. At $\sqrt{s}=5.1~\mathrm{GeV}$, the six light-neutrino cross sections span $9.28\times10^{-49}$--$1.78\times10^{-45}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$ for $m_{ββ}=30~\mathrm{meV}$ and $Δq^2=10^{-2}~\mathrm{GeV}^2$. For heavy-neutrino exchange, the cross sections scale with $η_N^2$, where $η_N=|U_{eN}|^2/m_N$. The leading propagator correction remains below one percent for $m_N\gtrsim50~\mathrm{GeV}$ over the phase-space region considered. At $\sqrt{s}=5.1~\mathrm{GeV}$, the heavy-neutrino cross sections span $7.55\times10^{-45}$--$1.98\times10^{-41}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$ for $η_N=4.0\times10^{-9}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$. At higher energies, the two exchange cases give the same CKM-driven channel ordering, with the $nn$ cross section largest. At fixed $\sqrt{s}$, compared with the light-neutrino spectra calculated with $Δq^2=10^{-2}~\mbox{GeV}^2$, the heavy-neutrino peaks occur at a smaller squared invariant mass for the proton pair and a larger one for the electron pair. These results extend the free-baryon analogue of neutrinoless double-beta decay to strange initial states and provide theoretical reference results for comparing light- and heavy-Majorana-neutrino exchange.

</details>


### [124] [Confronting $b \to (s, d)μ^+ μ^-$ and $\mathcal{L}_{K^{*}\bar{K}^{*}}$ puzzle in a $U(1)_{B-3L_μ}$ framework with neutrino phenomenology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34525)
*Dhiren Panda,Priya Mishra,Manas Kumar Mohapatra,Rukmani Mohanta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于U(1)_{B-3L_μ}模型的Z′新物理框架，以解释b→(s,d)μ⁺μ⁻衰变中与标准模型预测的偏差，并研究其在非轻子过程B_{s(d)}→K* K*及中微子现象学中的关联效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释b→(s,d)μ⁺μ⁻过程中观测到的与标准模型预测的偏差，探索可能的新物理来源。

Method: 构建基于U(1)_{B-3L_μ}规范对称性的Z′模型，结合b→(s,d)μ⁺μ⁻实验约束，分析其对B_{s(d)}→K* K*非轻子衰变及中微子物理的关联预言。

Result: 发现同时含矢量和轴矢量耦合（符号相反）的方案比纯矢量耦合更符合数据；该框架可自然关联解释轻子味破坏与非轻子衰变异常，并给出可检验的中微子物理预言。

Conclusion: U(1)_{B-3L_μ} Z′模型不仅能协调b→(s,d)μ⁺μ⁻反常，还通过多通道关联检验展现出强解释力，是值得深入研究的新物理候选框架。

Abstract: Current data on several observables in $b \to (s,d)$ $μ^+ μ^-$ transitions reveal certain discrepancies with Standard Model (SM) predictions. Considering the possibility that these deviations stem from new physics, we explore a $Z^{\prime}$ framework based on $U(1)_{B-3L_μ}$ model. The parameter space is examined in light of existing constraints from $b \to (s,d)$ $μ^+ μ^-$ transitions. Within this framework, we show that the quark couplings accommodating $b \to (s,d)$ $μ^+ μ^-$ anomalies, imprint correlated signatures on the non-leptonic puzzles $\mathcal{L}_{K^{*}\bar{K}^{*}}$ in $B_{s(d)} \to K^{*}\bar{K}^{*}$ decays. We find that the scenario with both vector and axial-vector contributions of opposite sign is favored by the data compared to pure vector contribution, offering a correlated probe of the underlying new-physics scenario. We further explore the neutrino phenomenology within the same framework in light of current data.

</details>


### [125] [Rational terms and UV counterterms of the SMEFT at next-to-leading order in QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34531)
*Giuseppe Ventura*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了在QCD下NLO精度的SMEFT维度六算符的有理项R2和紫外抵消项，适用于任意味结构、复数Wilson系数、重夸克及一般CKM矩阵，并给出相关Mathematica工具包与MadGraph5应用示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在NLO精度下精确研究超出标准模型的新物理效应，需系统处理SMEFT中维度六算符的紫外发散与有理项，尤其在存在味混合、重夸克和复数Wilson系数等现实情形下。

Method: 采用反交换γ5方案，对含单个γ5的闭合费米子圈进行规范不变处理；完成全味通用的NLO QCD圈图计算；推导夸克场在味混合下的on-shell重整化因子；结果以电子形式和Mathematica包发布。

Result: 给出了SMEFT维度六算符完整的NLO有理项R2和紫外抵消项；提供了夸克场on-shell重整化因子；开发了配套Mathematica软件包，并成功集成至MadGraph5_aMC@NLO用于NLO过程模拟。

Conclusion: 本工作为SMEFT在NLO精度下的唯象分析提供了首个全味通用、严格处理γ5与味混合的完整QCD修正框架，显著提升了新物理信号理论预测的可靠性与灵活性。

Abstract: We compute the rational terms $R_2$ and the ultraviolet counterterms of the dimension-six Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) at next-to-leading order (NLO) in QCD without any flavour assumption, for complex Wilson coefficients, massive quarks and a general CKM matrix. We also compute the on-shell renormalisation factors of the quark fields in the presence of flavour mixing. We work with an anticommuting $γ_5$ and read closed fermion loops with a single $γ_5$ at the operator insertion. The results, also for common flavour assumptions, are provided in electronic form and in a Mathematica package attached to the paper. We use them to build example models for MadGraph5_aMC@NLO and give NLO predictions for representative processes.

</details>


### [126] [Gravity-induced mixing, regeneration and CP violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34595)
*Jean-Marcel Rax*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出引力诱导的CP破坏机制，解释中性介子振荡中的CP破坏现象，并与实验数据高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解释中性介子实验中观测到的CP破坏现象及其起源，并解决强CP问题和中性K介子中时间反演破坏的小效应。

Method: 分析地球引力对中性介子振荡的影响，引入味振荡与夸克zitterbewegung之间的微弱引力耦合，进而推导质量本征态与CP本征态的偏离。

Result: 预测的CP破坏参数与实验数据完全一致；同时澄清了时间反演破坏和强CP问题。

Conclusion: 引力诱导的CP破坏为理解中性介子中CP对称性破缺提供了精确框架，并深刻影响对宇宙早期重子不对称生成机制的认识。

Abstract: The analysis of neutral mesons oscillations perturbed by Earth's gravity reveals a small coupling between flavor oscillations and quarks zitterbewegung. As a result of this small gravity-induced coupling between two very low energy oscillations, the mass eigenstates are no longer the Charge-Parity eigenstates. The three types of observed CP violation effects result from this gravity-induced mixing. The associated violation parameters are predicted in full agreement with the experimental data. Thus gravity-induced CP violation offers the accurate framework to understand the origin of CP symmetry violation in neutral mesons experiments. This origin renews in depth the problem of the required large-scale asymmetric baryogenesis in the early universe. Furthermore, the issues of the small time reversal violation in neutral kaons experiments and of the strong CP problem are fully clarified within this new framework of analysis taking into account gravity.

</details>


### [127] [Entanglement entropy in low x Deep Inelastic Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34604)
*Martin Hentschinski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了低x深度非弹性散射（DIS）系统中密度矩阵如何导致混合态及有限纠缠熵，并基于Levin-Lublinsky演化方程的数值解，给出了低x偶极子多重数的最新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Kharzeev和Levin 2017年工作的启发，探究质子波函数中纠缠的测量——即由末态强子多重数提取的强子熵。

Method: 通过分析低x DIS系统的密度矩阵性质，推导其混合态特征与纠缠熵；结合Levin-Lublinsky演化方程进行数值求解，计算低x偶极子多重数。

Result: 证实低x DIS系统密度矩阵可自然导致混合态和有限纠缠熵；获得基于Levin-Lublinsky方程的低x偶极子多重数数值结果。

Conclusion: 强子熵可作为质子波函数纠缠的有效探针，低x DIS中的量子纠缠可通过密度矩阵和偶极子模型定量刻画。

Abstract: In a 2017 paper by Kharzeev and Levin, it has been proposed that hadronic entropy, extracted from the multiplicity of final state hadrons, provides a measure of entanglement in the proton wave function, observed in Deep Inelastic Scattering (DIS) of electrons and protons. In this contribution we discuss how the density matrix of the low x DIS system can give rise to a mixed state and therefore to finite entanglement entropy. We further present recent numerical results on the determination of low x dipole multiplicities, based on the solution of the Levin-Lublinsky evolution equation.

</details>


### [128] [Tackling the DVCS inverse problem with finite element method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34631)
*Marija Čuić,Cédric Mezrag,José Manuel Morgado Chávez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有限元方法的新框架，以解决大虚度下2→2排他过程（如DVCS、TCS和DVMP）中阴影广义部分子分布（shadow GPDs）的非可逆性问题，从而消除反卷积歧义，并引入(a,p)正则化参数对GPD动量分数依赖进行系统改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 五年前提出的shadow GPD问题成为理论不确定性的主要来源，其影响可能远超实验不确定性，亟需解决。

Method: 采用有限元方法（FEM），将原本非可逆的反卷积问题转化为在有限离散面积a下病态但可解的问题，并引入离散面积a与局部多项式阶数p构成的正则化框架。

Result: 成功消除了deconvolution歧义；导出了理论最小离散面积a_min，对应GPD动量分数重构的最大理论分辨率；建立了(a,p)正则化方案，使GPD动量依赖描述能随pQCD硬核计算阶数系统提升。

Conclusion: 有限元方法为shadow GPD问题提供了优雅且实用的解决方案，(a,p)参数框架兼具物理直观性与数学严谨性，显著提升了GPD理论重建的精度与可控性。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the problem of shadow GPDs, arising from the non-invertibility of the measured $2\to 2$ exclusive processes at large virtualities (DVCS, TCS and DVMP). This problem arose five years ago as the main source of theoretical uncertainties, potentially dominating experimental ones by orders of magnitude. We show here that the deconvolution ambiguity can be elegantly eliminated by relying on finite element method. The problem is essentially converted at any finite discretisation area $a$ from a non-invertible to an ill-conditioned one. Given experimental uncertainties, this yields a theoretical lower bound on the discretisation area $a_\textrm{min}$, that can be interpreted as the maximal theoretical resolution one can obtain on the reconstruction of the lightcone momentum fractions. Associated with the local polynomial degree $p$, the pair $(a,p)$ provides an insightful and easily understandable regularisation framework, allowing one to systematically improve the description of the momentum fractions dependence of GPDs with the pQCD order at which the hard kernels of exclusive processes are computed.

</details>


### [129] [QED corrections to neutron and nuclear $β$-decay at order $α^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34804)
*Zehua Cao,Richard J. Hill,Ryan Plestid,Peter Vander Griend*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了中子和核β衰变的两圈QED修正，包含完整的电子质量依赖性和实辐射贡献，显著降低了低能辐射修正的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高中子和核β衰变中低能辐射修正的精度，减小理论不确定性。

Method: 计算两圈QED修正，包含完整电子质量依赖性和实辐射贡献。

Result: 中子的辐射修正结果为δ_{R,static} = (29.199 ± 0.001)×10^{-3}，相比之前在大能量/小电子质量极限下的结果，不确定性降低了约70倍；超允许核衰变中Z无关的α²阶修正为(0.1–0.5)×10^{-4}。

Conclusion: 该工作大幅提升了中子β衰变辐射修正的精度，并为超允许核衰变提供了与当前不确定度相当的高阶QED修正估计。

Abstract: We compute two-loop QED corrections to neutron and nuclear beta decay, including full electron mass dependence and real radiation contributions. The result for the neutron, $δ_{R,\,\rm static}= ( 29.199 \pm 0.001 )\times 10^{-3}$, implies a factor $\sim 70 $ reduction in the uncertainty of the low-energy radiative corrections compared to previous results obtained in the large energy/small electron-mass limit, $m_e/(m_n-m_p) \to 0$. For superallowed nuclear decays we find that the $Z$-independent order $α^2$ corrections range from $(0.1-0.5) \times 10^{-4}$ i.e., of roughly the expected size and comparable to current uncertainty estimates.

</details>


### [130] [Probing Ultralight Dark Matter at the Electroweak Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34808)
*Martin Bauer,Sreemanti Chakraborti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了超轻暗物质通过误对齐机制产生振荡信号，并评估了当前及未来对撞机在Z和希格斯玻色子宽度测量以及Z→τ⁺τ⁻衰变中CP奇观测量（如电偶极矩）方面的灵敏度，发现其对超轻暗物质的探测能力优于原子钟和等效原理检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻暗物质可解释全尺度上的天体物理暗物质证据，但需通过误对齐机制产生观测到的宇宙物质能量密度，从而引发时变振荡信号；需寻找高灵敏度探测手段。

Method: 分析当前与未来对撞机对Z和希格斯玻色子宽度的测量灵敏度，并重点研究Z→τ⁺τ⁻衰变中CP奇观（如电偶极矩）作为探针的有效性。

Result: Z→τ⁺τ⁻衰变中的CP奇观（如电偶极矩）对超轻暗物质的探测灵敏度优于原子钟和等效原理实验。

Conclusion: 高能对撞机中的特定CP奇观测量是探测超轻暗物质的一种强有力新途径，有望超越传统低能精密测量方法。

Abstract: Ultralight dark matter provides an explanation for astrophysical evidence for dark matter at all scales. This non-thermal dark matter requires a misalignment mechanism to reproduce the observed matter energy density of the Universe and therefore induce oscillating, time-dependent signals. We determine the sensitivity of current and future collider measurements of the width of the Z and Higgs bosons, and show that CP odd observables such as dipole moments in $Z\to τ^+τ^-$ decays provide a better sensitivity to ultralight dark matter than atomic clocks or equivalence principle tests.

</details>


### [131] [Heavy-flavor transverse energy-energy correlators in the color-glass condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34815)
*Zhong-Bo Kang,Carlos Lamas,Jani Penttala*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Color Glass Condensate框架下，首次在领头阶精确计算了质子-核碰撞中前向重夸克对产生的横向能量-能量关联函数（TEEC），保留了重夸克质量、末态部分子精确运动学及全方位角依赖性，并通过动量求和律消除了碎裂函数的主导依赖；结果表明核靶导致TEEC整体压低并使角分布偏离背对背构型，其效应依赖于夸克质量、运动学及核几何，凸显了重味能量关联对核小-x动力学的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究核环境中小-x区域QCD动力学对重夸克能量关联的影响，克服传统背对背近似与碎裂函数强依赖的局限。

Method: 在Color Glass Condensate框架下，基于领头阶微扰计算，严格保留重夸克质量、末态部分子精确运动学与全方位角依赖；采用共线碎裂方案，并利用动量求和律对所有强子种类求和以消除碎裂函数。

Result: 发现相对于质子-质子碰撞，核靶导致每核子积分TEEC压低，并使相对方位角分布向远离背对背方向偏移；该修正幅度与依赖关系随夸克质量（c/b）、碰撞能量（RHIC/LHC）及核几何而变。

Conclusion: TEEC是探测核内小-x动力学的灵敏探针，且其对碎裂过程的主导依赖被显著削弱，提升了理论预言的可靠性。

Abstract: We calculate the transverse energy--energy correlator (TEEC) for forward heavy-quark pair production in proton--nucleus collisions at leading order within the Color Glass Condensate framework. The calculation retains the heavy--quark mass, exact final-state parton kinematics and full relative azimuthal-angle dependence, without imposing a back-to-back approximation. Within the adopted collinear fragmentation prescription, a complete sum over hadron species in the two heavy-quark fragmentation branches eliminates the fragmentation functions through the momentum sum rule. We present numerical results for charm production at RHIC energies and charm and bottom production at LHC energies, using HERA-constrained McLerran--Venugopalan initial conditions evolved with the running-coupling Balitsky--Kovchegov equation. Relative to proton--proton collisions, the nuclear target suppresses the integrated TEEC per nucleon and shifts the relative angular weight towards configurations farther from back-to-back. The magnitude and angular dependence of these modifications vary with the quark mass, collision kinematics and nuclear geometry. These results illustrate the sensitivity of heavy-flavor energy correlations to nuclear small-$x$ dynamics while reducing their leading collinear fragmentation dependence.

</details>


### [132] [Screening of New Abelian Long-Range Forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34838)
*Marco Costa,Andrey Shkerin,Ananda Thomlinson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了当U(1)新力的媒介粒子质量由希格斯机制产生时，对B-L及L_a-L_b（a,b=e,μ,τ）等标准模型对称性破缺所引致的长程力实验限制的影响；发现天体的大重子/轻子电荷可导致希格斯场偏离真空值，从而引起电荷屏蔽效应；该屏蔽效应可显著削弱等效原理检验、中微子振荡实验对新力强度与媒介粒子质量的限制，并完全消除中子星双星系统对μ子型L_μ-L_τ力的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标准模型扩展中新型U(1)长程力在希格斯机制赋予媒介粒子质量情形下的可观测性，特别是天文尺度上希格斯场响应对传统实验约束的影响。

Method: 理论分析希格斯场在带大B或L电荷天体附近的非线性响应与屏蔽效应，结合等效原理检验、中微子振荡及中子星双星动力学等实验/观测约束，定量评估其对U(1)力参数空间的修正。

Result: 发现希格斯介导的U(1)力因电荷屏蔽效应导致实验限制显著放宽：等效原理与中微子振荡实验约束减弱，而中子星双星对L_μ-L_τ力的排除界限完全消失。

Conclusion: 希格斯机制引入的非线性场响应可实质性改变新型长程力的天文-粒子物理联合约束，提示未来理论建模与实验解释需纳入此类介质屏蔽效应。

Abstract: We study how bounds on new $U(1)$ long-range forces, resulting from gauging the Standard Model symmetries $B-L$ and $L_a-L_b$, $a,b=e,μ,τ$, are modified if the mediator mass is generated by the Higgs mechanism. Large baryonic and leptonic charges of astronomical bodies displace the Higgs field from its vacuum value, resulting in charge screening. Depending on the $U(1)$ Higgs mass, the screening relaxes the bounds on the new force strength and mediator mass from equivalence principle violation and neutrino oscillations experiments, and eliminates the bounds on the muonic $L_μ-L_τ$ force from neutron star binaries.

</details>


### [133] [Probing the CKM Origin of CP Violation with Paramagnetic EDMs and Rare Kaon Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34856)
*Giancarlo D'Ambrosio,Luca Di Luzio*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了标准模型中参数化电偶极矩（EDMs）的起源，指出尽管长程双光子交换效应显著，但其CP守恒特性与核子耦合中CP破坏的企鹅图分量结合后，电磁修正仍保持在百分之几水平，因此EDMs仍能有效测量CKM矩阵元Im(V_{ts}^*V_{td})，并与稀有介子衰变共同确定同一CKM平面虚部坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解参数化EDMs的物理起源及其与CKM矩阵的关系，特别是区分短程与长程贡献，并评估其对CP破坏和新物理探测的敏感性。

Method: 理论分析，结合标准模型中kaon交换、双光子交换机制及CP对称性约束，估算各贡献相对大小，并关联到稀有介子衰变过程。

Result: 确认双光子交换虽大但CP守恒，需与微小CP破坏的企鹅型核子耦合配对，使电磁修正可控；EDMs与K_S→μ⁺μ⁻、K_L→π⁰νν̄共同确定同一CKM虚部坐标，不依赖B衰变输入。

Conclusion: 参数化EDMs是检验CKM机制统一性与探查轻子特异性新物理的重要工具，需分子EDM实验、LHCb/NA62/KOTO稀有介子实验及格点QCD计算协同推进。

Abstract: In the Standard Model, paramagnetic electric dipole moments (EDMs) are dominated by kaon exchange, which pairs the CP-violating short-distance transition $s\to d\,e^+e^-$ with a CP-conserving weak kaon-nucleon coupling. Long-distance two-photon exchange, which dominates $K_L\to e^+e^-$, gives an electron vertex about thirty-five times larger than the short-distance one and could obscure this origin. We show that it does not: the two-photon vertex conserves CP and must be paired with the CP-violating penguin component of the nucleon coupling, whose relative size is $3\times10^{-4}$, so that electromagnetic corrections stay at the few-percent level. Paramagnetic EDMs therefore measure $\mathrm{Im}(V_{ts}^*V_{td})$ and, together with the CP-violating $S$ wave of $K_S\toμ^+μ^-$ and $K_L\toπ^0ν\barν$, determine the same imaginary coordinate of a kaon CKM plane with electrons, muons, and neutrinos, without $B$-decay input. Lepton-specific new physics, or CP violation that bypasses kaon exchange, would separate these determinations. Testing their common CKM origin calls for coordinated progress in molecular EDM searches, rare-kaon experiments at LHCb, NA62, and KOTO, and lattice determinations of weak nucleon matrix elements.

</details>


### [134] [The Look-Elsewhere Effect in Searches for New Physics: from Classical Methods to Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34888)
*Gaia Grosso,Sergio Rodriguez-Benitez,Louis Lyons*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了高能物理中寻找新物理时的“四处查看效应”（LEE）问题，涵盖其统计基础、连续与离散参数扫描的校正方法、机器学习引入的选择性偏差及其与选择性推断的联系，并指出当前开放问题与报告建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在参数空间未预先指定位置时，因多区域扫描导致显著性膨胀的问题；尤其关注传统方法在机器学习搜索中的不适用性及隐含的后选择偏差。

Method: 回顾Davies-Gross-Vitells框架，分析连续与离散多重检验的校正方法，结合数值示例；探讨ML模型分辨率与表达能力如何影响LEE，并关联选择性推断理论。

Result: 厘清LEE定义模糊性（如'elsewhere'范围），揭示ML搜索中隐式后选择偏差的本质，指出经验校准计算代价高、缺乏ML检验统计量解析捷径等关键挑战。

Conclusion: 需发展适用于ML的新LEE校正方法与高效计算策略，并规范新物理发现显著性的报告方式，兼顾经典与ML驱动的粒子物理搜索。

Abstract: The look-elsewhere effect (LEE) arises when the location of an effect in parameter space is not specified in advance: scanning many locations inflates the significance of the largest excess, and the local $p$-value must be corrected into a global one. A well-established framework, rooted in the work of Davies and operationalised for particle physics by Gross and Vitells, provides this correction for scans over continuous parameters. Some aspects, however, remain unsettled. The definition of "elsewhere" is ambiguous and ranges from a single distribution to an entire experimental programme. Moreover, the analytical shortcuts of the classical framework do not carry over to searches based on machine learning (ML), which are affected by an implicit post-selection bias that depends on the model's resolution and expressiveness. This review covers the LEE in searches for new physics at colliders, from its statistical foundations to these modern challenges. We discuss the corrections for continuous scans and for multiple discrete tests, illustrated with simple numerical examples, and the interaction between ML and the LEE, which we connect to the broader literature on selective inference. We highlight open problems, including the computational cost of empirical calibration for complex models and the development of analytical shortcuts for ML test statistics, and offer recommendations for reporting discovery significance in both classical and ML-based searches in particle physics.

</details>


### [135] [KEBBABS: Kilometric Enhanced Balloon-Baseline for Axion Birefringence Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34927)
*Tommaso Feri,Silvia Gasparotto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用平流层气球间自由空间激光链路，通过探测轴子-光子耦合引起的激光偏振旋转来宽带搜寻超轻轴子暗物质的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服传统谐振光学腔需多次反射的限制，并拓展对超轻轴子暗物质（质量范围4×10⁻¹⁵–10⁻¹¹ eV）的探测能力，尤其是突破现有实验（如CAST）和天体物理限制。

Method: 部署两个在30–40 km高空飞行的平流层气球构成自由空间光学链路：一个发射线偏振激光，另一个反射回第一气球上的偏振仪；利用长基线增强灵敏度，并规避低频（<1 Hz）下吊舱滚动引入的假信号。

Result: 在mₐ=10⁻¹⁴ eV处，'Pathfinder'方案可达到gₐᵧ≈2.8×10⁻¹¹ GeV⁻¹（优于CAST限约2倍）；'Feasible today'方案接近当前天体物理上限；'Futuristic'方案可达gₐᵧ≈6.4×10⁻¹⁵ GeV⁻¹（比天体物理限低约两个数量级）。

Conclusion: 该气球基偏振测量方案为超轻轴子暗物质提供了有前景的宽带探测新途径，具备分阶段实现、逐步逼近理论极限的能力。

Abstract: We propose a broadband search for ultralight axion dark matter through the rotation of laser polarization induced by the axion--photon coupling. Two stratospheric balloons flying at altitudes of 30--40 km form a free-space optical link. One balloon sends a linearly polarized beam to the other, which reflects it back to a polarimeter on the first balloon. The long physical baseline provides an alternative to the repeated reflections used in resonant optical cavities. We target axion masses in the range $4\times10^{-15}\,\mathrm{eV}\lesssim m_a\lesssim10^{-11}\,\mathrm{eV}$. The lower edge corresponds to a frequency of about $1\,\mathrm{Hz}$, below which gondola roll about the line of sight can mimic a polarization signal. At $m_a=10^{-14}\,\mathrm{eV}$, a low-cost Pathfinder using flight-demonstrated technology could probe couplings as small as $g_{aγ}\simeq2.8\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$, about a factor of two below the CAST bound. At the same mass, the Feasible today configuration could probe couplings just below the astrophysical bounds considered here, while the Futuristic configuration could reach $g_{aγ}\simeq6.4\times10^{-15}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$, roughly two orders of magnitude below those bounds.

</details>
