### [136] [Constraining Intergalactic Dust with Supernova Color Evolution: Current Constraints from DES and Forecasts for LSST and Roman](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33276)
*Erik R. Peterson,Dillon Brout,Daniel Scolnic,Bastien Carreres,Rebecca C. Chen,Richard Kessler,Brodie Popovic,Benjamin M. Rose,Maria Vincenzi,Maria Acevedo,John Franklin Crenshaw,Lluís Galbany,Matthew Grayling,Benne W. Holwerda,Jacqueline McCleary,Ayan Mitra,Rujuta Purohit,Corentin Ravoux,Masao Sako,Bruno O. Sánchez,Christopher W. Stubbs,Syed Uddin,The LSST Dark Energy Science Collaboration,The Roman Science Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了星系际尘埃对Ia型超新星（SN Ia）宇宙学测量的影响，指出当前观测数据（如DES）尚无法有效约束星系际尘埃，但未来Roman空间望远镜有望以>3σ置信度限制其模型；LSST虽统计量大，但仅能达到<2σ；星系际尘埃会显著降低不同暗能量模型的精度（FoM），但不能解释近期关于暗能量演化的迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系际尘埃作为Ia型超新星宇宙学中的重要系统误差，其影响尚未被充分约束，而银河系和宿主星系尘埃已有较多研究，因此需评估其对高红移SN距离模数及暗能量参数测量的影响。

Method: 基于Ménard等人（2010）的估计，结合SN模拟与颜色红移演化特征，利用DES、LSST和Roman等巡天数据的统计能力，对多种星系际尘埃参数化模型进行独立约束，并传播至wCDM和w0waCDM宇宙学参数拟合中评估其对Figure of Merit（FoM）的影响。

Result: Roman可对星系际尘埃实现>3σ约束，LSST仅<2σ；LSST的FoM在wCDM和w0waCDM模型中分别下降约60%和20%，Roman则分别下降约20%和5%；用Roman约束尘埃后可略微改善LSST的FoM；DES分析中尘埃不确定性导致FoM下降16.9%（wCDM）和8.1%（w0waCDM）；星系际尘埃无法解释暗能量演化迹象。

Conclusion: 星系际尘埃是不可忽略的系统误差，需借助高精度、多波段、大样本的未来巡天（如Roman）加以约束；单纯依赖大样本统计（如LSST）不足以解决该问题；其影响虽显著，但不改变当前暗能量演化趋势的基本结论。

Abstract: Dust is an important systematic in precision Type Ia supernova (SN Ia) cosmology, and while dust in the Milky Way and SN host galaxies has been extensively studied, the impact of potential intergalactic dust is less well constrained. Using estimates from B. Ménard et al. (2010), we find that current intergalactic dust bounds could shift SN distance moduli by up to 0.02 mag at $z \gtrsim 1$ from simulations. However, we show dust models can be constrained independently by SN samples themselves via a specific predicted redshift-dependent evolution observed in SN colors. While current data from the Dark Energy Survey (DES) cannot distinguish between various amounts of intergalactic dust in our universe, we demonstrate that the Roman Space Telescope (Roman) will place a significant constraint on intergalactic dust models ($>$ 3$σ$). In contrast, the Rubin Observatory's Legacy Survey of Space and Time (LSST), even with its unprecedented statistics, will only reach $\lesssim$ 2$σ$ significance on any given intergalactic dust prediction relative to no dust. We propagate multiple parameterizations of intergalactic dust through to cosmology. For LSST, we see a $\sim$60% reduction in the figure of merit (FoM) for $w$CDM and a $\sim$20% reduction for $w_0w_a$CDM, and for Roman, a $\sim$20% reduction in the FoM for $w$CDM and a $\sim$5% reduction for $w_0w_a$CDM. Utilizing Roman as an external constraint on dust for LSST results in a slightly improved FoM ($\sim$50% reduction) for $w$CDM for LSST. Finally, we show that intergalactic dust uncertainties decrease the FoM of the previous DES analysis (16.9% for $w$CDM and 8.1% for $w_0w_a$CDM) and that intergalactic dust cannot explain away recent hints for evolving dark energy.

</details>


### [137] [CHEX-MATE: Probing the thermal$-$non-thermal connection in radio halo clusters with LoTSS-DR2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33293)
*M. Balboni,F. Gastaldello,A. Bonafede,Y. E. Bahar,I. Bartalucci,A. Botteon,H. Bourdin,G. Brunetti,R. Cassano,F. De Luca,S. Ettori,M. Gaspari,M. Gitti,A. Ignesti,C. Jones,L. Lovisari,P. Mazzotta,E. Pointecouteau,G. W. Pratt,M. Rossetti,J. Sayers,M. Sereno,R. J. van Weeren,S. De Grandi,S. Ghizzardi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过结合CHEX-MATE和LOFAR巡天数据，对18个具有射电晕的星系团开展首项均匀、空间分辨的射电–X射线联合研究，发现射电与X射线表面亮度呈显著亚线性相关，且该关系随半径和动力学状态（扰动/平静）变化，支持湍流再加速模型，并表明热与非热等离子体存在强耦合演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系团中热等离子体（X射线辐射）与非热成分（射电晕，含相对论电子与磁场）之间的物理关联与协同演化机制，需高精度、空间分辨的多波段观测。

Method: 基于CHEX-MATE（X射线）与LOFAR T2SS DR2（射电）数据，对18个射电晕星系团进行统一的空间分辨分析；计算射电与X射线表面亮度的相关性（Spearman系数）、I_R–I_X关系斜率及其径向分布；按动力学状态分类比较，并利用X射线信息对射电轮廓进行归一化缩放。

Result: 1) 射电与X射线表面亮度强相关（r_s ≥ 0.5），67±5%样本呈亚线性关系；2) 斜率径向行为因动力学状态而异：扰动型星系团斜率符合湍流再加速模型预测（恒定宇宙线/热能比），宁静型则需该比值随半径增大；3) 利用X射线信息归一化射电轮廓可显著降低散射，揭示与热ICM相似的动力学分类趋势。

Conclusion: 热与非热等离子体在星系团中紧密耦合、协同演化，其相互作用受宏观动力学状态和微观物理过程共同调控；射电–X射线联合分析是揭示该耦合本质的关键途径。

Abstract: The main baryonic component of galaxy clusters is in the form of a hot plasma observed in the X-rays. In addition, clusters host a non-thermal component (relativistic particles and magnetic fields) which is studied mainly via radio telescopes and is dynamically coupled to the thermal plasma. These two components evolve together within the system and are subject to both micro and macro-physical processes which can be traced only with deep and detailed observations. We present the first homogeneous spatially resolved radio--X-ray study of 18 radio halo clusters combining CHEX-MATE and the LOFAR Two metre Sky Survey DR2. Radio ($I_R$) and X-ray ($I_X$) surface brightness are clearly correlated (Spearman coefficient $r_s \ge 0.5$), following a typically sublinear $I_R-I_X$ relation ($67 \pm 5\%$ of the sample) that appears to be independent of other cluster properties, although a tentative trend with mass/redshift may be worth exploring with larger samples. When inspecting such a relation as a function of the radius, we observe a variety of behaviours, which suggest that the interplay between the thermal and non-thermal plasma components varies with radius. We find the averaged slope profile of dynamically disturbed clusters to be consistent with expectations from turbulent re-acceleration models where a constant ratio between cosmic-ray electron and thermal energy densities is assumed. Differently, less perturbed clusters require an increasing cosmic-ray-to-thermal energy ratio with radius. Finally, we show that radio halo surface-brightness profiles can be effectively rescaled using X-ray information and theoretical expectations. This procedure significantly reduces their scatter and reveals common underlying trends based on the cluster dynamics, resembling what is observed for the thermal ICM, and strengthening the idea of a strong interplay between these two plasma components.

</details>


### [138] [Acoustic Priors from the Latest Cosmic Microwave Background Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33334)
*Kushal Lodha,Jun-Qian Jiang,Purba Mukherjee,Arman Shafieloo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了CMB声学峰的物理机制，分离早期宇宙信息与晚期引力透镜、ISW效应等污染，利用多组CMB数据（Planck PR4、ACT DR6、SPT-3G）联合透镜重建，给出了更精确、模型无关的声学角尺度和物质密度参数约束，并探讨了其对晚时宇宙学模型（如w0waCDM）和BAO观测一致性的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMB声学峰中混杂着早期宇宙信息与晚期效应（如引力透镜、ISW），需将其分离以获得更可靠、模型无关的宇宙学参数约束，并评估不同晚时模型与观测（如BAO、SN）的一致性。

Method: 发展并应用一种物理方法，将CMB温度与极化功率谱中的早期声学信号与晚期透镜/ISW效应解耦；联合Planck PR4、ACT DR6、SPT-3G数据及联合透镜重建进行参数估计；采用声学先验预测BAO距离，并对比DESI等观测。

Result: 获得更新的模型无关参数：100θ∗=1.04100±0.00021，ωb=(2.2440±0.0099)×10⁻²，ωbc=0.1410±0.0011；ACT加入后使ωb和ns偏高；透镜谱在L≈50和L∼10³处存在超出ΛCDM的特征；考虑透镜平滑自由度后，w0waCDM相对于ΛCDM的统计偏好显著下降（3.0σ→1.9σ）；CMB声学先验预测的BAO距离与DESI测量在标准预重组物理下基本一致。

Conclusion: 早期CMB信息可被有效提取并用于强健的模型无关宇宙学推断；晚期效应（尤其是透镜）建模方式显著影响晚时模型选择结果；当前CMB+BAO数据组合在标准物理假设下未显示对ΛCDM的强偏离，支持其作为基准模型的有效性。

Abstract: We revisit the physics of the acoustic peaks and illustrate how early-Universe information in the cosmic microwave background (CMB) can be disentangled from late-time signatures imprinted by gravitational lensing and the integrated Sachs-Wolfe (ISW) effect. Applying this separation to Planck PR4, ACT DR6, and SPT-3G D1 temperature and polarization data combined with the joint lensing reconstruction, we update the model-independent constraints on the angular acoustic scale and the physical densities, $100θ_*=1.04100\pm0.00021$, $ω_{\rm b}=(2.2440\pm0.0099)\times10^{-2}$, and $ω_{\rm bc}=0.1410\pm0.0011$. These constraints are consistent with the previous Planck PR4-only analysis and remain stable for extended late-time cosmologies beyond $Λ$CDM. Adding ACT DR6 to the combination favors a higher $ω_{\rm b}$ and, through their correlation, a higher primordial tilt $n_{\rm s}$ than PR4 alone. The reconstructed lensing spectrum exceeds that of $Λ$CDM, with two features near $L\simeq50$ and $L\sim10^3$, and the excess is reduced when WMAP replaces the Planck primary spectra. Marginalizing over the lensing-induced peak smoothing, rather than tying it to the late-time model, lowers the preference of the CMB and DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO) for $w_0w_a$CDM over $Λ$CDM from $3.0σ$ to $1.9σ$, or from $3.2σ$ to $2.4σ$ when Union3.1 supernovae are added. We further use the acoustic prior to predict BAO distances from CMB data alone under several late-time expansion histories, testing their consistency with DESI measurements assuming standard pre-recombination physics.

</details>


### [139] [No Cluster Left Behind I: Joint Mass Calibration for Dark Energy Survey and South Pole Telescope Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33522)
*C. Zhou,A. N. Salcedo,S. Grandis,T. Jeltema,M. Costanzi,L. E. Bleem,A. Farahi,M. Gatti,T. Shin,F. Kéruzoré,C. L. Reichardt,J. H. Esteves,M. Aguena,S. Allam,D. Bacon,J. Blazek,S. Bocquet,D. Brooks,D. L. Burke,R. Camilleri,J. Carretero,L. N. da Costa,T. M. Davis,D. L. DePoy,S. Desai,P. Doel,A. E. Evrard,J. García-Bellido,D. Gruen,G. Gutierrez,D. L. Hollowood,D. J. James,K. Kuehn,O. Lahav,J. L. Marshall,J. Mena-Fernández,R. Miquel,R. L. C. Ogando,A. A. Plazas Malagón,A. Porredon,A. K. Romer,E. Sanchez,D. Sanchez Cid,I. Sevilla-Noarbe,E. Suchyta,M. E. C. Swanson,V. Vikram,N. Weaverdyck,J. Weller*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种灵活的多波段星系团联合建模框架，整合光学（DES）与SZ效应（SPT）数据，同时校准质量并自洽约束光学选择偏差，发现约20%的大尺度引力透镜信号偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决光学识别星系团因视线投影效应导致的弱引力透镜质量定标选择偏差问题，以及ICM（X射线/SZ）选样受限于高质​​量系统的局限，实现多波段星系团数据的充分利用。

Method: 构建一个可联合建模多波段（如光学richness、弱引力透镜、SZ信号）观测数据的统计框架，允许仅在单一巡天中被探测到的星系团参与分析；以DES和SPT数据为试点，联合拟合richness、弱透镜信号与SZ信号。

Result: 发现仅由richness选取的星系团存在约20%的大尺度透镜信号偏差；richness与SZ联合选取时，偏差从低richness处约-20%变化至高richness处为零；该框架具有高可扩展性与可拓展性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能更精确校准星系团质量并自洽处理选择效应，显著优于现有仅限高质​​量SZ检测样本的方法，为下一代巡天中多波段星系团数据分析提供了通用、高效的新范式。

Abstract: Multiwavelength observations of galaxy clusters are among the most promising probes of the growth of structure and dark energy. Optically identified clusters have a high number density and can thus probe the halo mass function well into the group regime, but their weak-lensing mass calibration suffers from a selection bias induced by line-of-sight projection effects. Clusters detected via their intracluster medium (ICM) -- through X-ray emission or the thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich effect (SZe) -- are less affected by such selection biases, but current survey sensitivity restricts ICM-selected samples to high-mass systems. Previous multiwavelength analyses tend to limit to clusters with millimeter detections and thus to the high-mass population. In this paper, we propose a flexible modeling framework in which all clusters from multiwavelength surveys can be combined and modeled, even if they are detected in only one survey. As a pilot study, we jointly model richness and lensing of clusters from the Dark Energy Survey and the SZe signal from the South Pole Telescope. This approach yields precise cluster mass calibration while self-consistently constraining optical selection biases. We have found a $\sim$20% bias in the large-scale lensing signal of clusters selected by richness, and a bias ranging from $\sim-20$% at low richness to zero at high richness for clusters selected by both richness and SZe. Compared to current approaches, this framework is highly scalable and readily extensible, paving the way for the full utilization of multiwavelength cluster data in next-generation surveys.

</details>


### [140] [Inference of Cosmic Dawn and Reionization Parameters from the 21 cm Global Spectrum Measurement of the DSL Mission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33541)
*Chengliang Xu,Meng Zhou,Furen Deng,Yidong Xu,Xuelei Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个贝叶斯推理框架，用于分析DSL（Hongmeng）任务在月球轨道上观测的21 cm全球谱数据，并结合21 cm空间功率谱数据，以约束宇宙黎明与再电离时期的天体物理参数。研究发现单独使用全球谱存在前景与信号简并性问题，而联合功率谱可显著打破简并、提升参数约束精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面21 cm观测受系统误差限制，DSL任务在月球轨道开展观测可规避此类误差，有望实现迄今最精确的21 cm全球谱测量；亟需建立可靠的数据分析框架以提取宇宙黎明与再电离时期的物理信息。

Method: 构建贝叶斯推理框架，采用物理驱动的模拟器21cmEMU统一生成全球谱与功率谱，对前景参数进行边缘化处理；分别假设DSL全球谱残差误差对应30/300/3000个轨道周期的热噪声，并引入HERA或SKA-Low的功率谱典型预报。

Result: 仅用全球谱数据时天体物理参数仍存在较强简并；加入空间功率谱后，参数约束显著收紧，二者高度互补；对吸收最小值位于105 MHz和94 MHz的两个典型模型均验证了该结论。

Conclusion: DSL的21 cm全球谱与下一代干涉阵列（如HERA/SKA-Low）的功率谱联合分析，是突破前景简并、高精度约束宇宙黎明与再电离物理参数的关键路径。

Abstract: The 21 cm global spectrum is an important observable for cosmic dawn and epoch of reionization. The Discovering the Sky at the Longest Wavelengths mission (DSL, also known as Hongmeng), will measure the 21 cm global spectrum from the lunar orbit, thus avoiding the systematic effects common in ground based experiments, potentially producing the most accurate measurement to date. In this paper, we present a Bayesian inference framework, and investigate the constraints that can be placed on the cosmic dawn and reionization model parameters, either from the 21 cm global spectrum data alone, or from its combination with 21\,cm spatial power-spectrum data. For the global spectrum data, we assume a residual error equivalent to the thermal noise of a total observation of 30, 300, or 3000 orbit cycles of DSL. For the power spectrum, we adopt representative forecasts for HERA or SKA-Low. We find that there is generally a degeneracy between the foreground and the 21 cm global signal, and one has to make some restrictions on the form of either the foreground or the 21 cm global spectrum to break this degeneracy. Using the physically motivated emulator 21cmEMU, we generate the 21 cm global spectrum and power spectrum under a common astrophysical parameterization, while marginalizing over the foreground parameters. We derive constraints for two representative models, with their global spectrum absorption minimum at 105 and 94 MHz respectively. With only the global spectrum data, there remains much degeneracy in the astrophysical parameters. We find that the spatial power spectrum is highly complementary to the global spectrum, which can break the degeneracy and tighten up the constraints significantly.

</details>


### [141] [Multiwavelength Analysis of the Merging Cluster RM J121917.6+505432.8: A Three-way Merger in Disguise?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33941)
*Rodrigo Stancioli,David Wittman,Kyle Finner,Faik Bouhrik,Zhuoran Gao*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过多波段观测确认星系团RM J121917.6+505432.8处于并合状态，弱引力透镜揭示双峰暗物质分布，X射线显示后近心点并合特征，光谱测得子团视向速度差为480 km/s；但东侧主子团高度拉长，引发是否为三体并合的疑问，模型拟合倾向三晕模型但统计证据不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确认RM J1219是否为双模并合星系团，并探究其暗物质与热气体结构，特别是其形态异常是否暗示更复杂的并合（如三体）场景。

Method: 结合HST/ACS光学成像（用于弱引力透镜质量重建）、XMM-Newton X射线成像（分析ICM形态）和Keck/Deimos光谱（测定子团红移与视向速度差），并采用多种NFW晕模型（球形/椭圆形、双晕/三晕组合）拟合弱透镜剪切数据。

Result: 弱透镜显示两个与最亮星系重合的质量峰；X射线呈现显著不对称、符合后近心点并合；测得东西子团视向速度差480 km/s；东侧子团高度拉长；三晕NFW模型拟合最优，但无法在统计上排除双晕模型。

Conclusion: RM J1219确为正在并合的星系团，但其东子团异常形态使‘双模’假设存疑；当前数据支持三体并合可能性，但尚不足以确证，需更高信噪比观测进一步验证。

Abstract: We identify the galaxy cluster RM J121917.6+505432.8 as a candidate bimodal merging cluster due to its optical properties listed in the redMaPPer cluster catalog. To confirm the cluster's merging state and reveal its dark matter halo and intracluster medium (ICM) morphologies, we conducted a multiwavelength follow-up campaign encompassing HST/ACS optical observations, XMM-Newton X-ray imaging, and Keck/Deimos multislit spectroscopy. The weak lensing mass reconstruction performed with the HST images shows two resolved peaks whose positions coincide with the two brightest cluster galaxies. The ICM morphology is consistent with a post-pericenter merger, with significant asymmetry in X-ray flux between subclusters. Our Keck/Deimos redshift measurements reveal a relative velocity $Δv_{\mathrm{LOS}} = 480$ km s$^{-1}$ between eastern and western subclusters. The eastern, more massive subcluster, however, exhibits a highly elongated shape, suggesting either a dark matter halo of considerable eccentricity or the presence of a third, unresolved halo, eliciting the question of whether RMJ1219 is truly binary or a three-way merger. To investigate these hypotheses, we fit the weak lensing shear data using four different combinations of spherical and elliptical NFW halo models and provide an estimate of the mass and concentration of each subhalo. Although the best fit is achieved with a three-halo model, the data are insufficient to determine whether the introduction of extra parameters is statistically justified.

</details>


### [142] [Halo spin-axis correlations in IllustrisTNG: Lagrangian-to-Eulerian separation mapping, baryonic effects, and redshift-space distortions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34111)
*Jun-Sung Moon,Teppei Okumura,Ue-Li Pen*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用IllustrisTNG模拟（TNG300）研究了暗物质晕与恒星自转轴之间的关联性，并基于线性潮汐扭矩理论（LTTT）构建模型进行解释；结果表明，当前观测到的自转轴关联性保留了初始潮汐场的印记，且可通过LTTT结合拉格朗日-欧拉分离映射较好地再现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暗物质晕和星系恒星成分的角动量起源及其在宇宙学尺度上的空间关联性，检验线性潮汐扭矩理论在非线性演化后的适用性。

Method: 分析TNG300模拟中z=0时DM晕与恒星自转轴的方向关联性；构建基于LTTT的理论模型，并引入由模拟直接测量得到的拉格朗日到欧拉分离映射关系；对比DM-only与hydro run、实空间与红移空间的结果。

Result: DM晕自转轴在拉格朗日空间存在正相关，LTTT模型能很好拟合其分离依赖关系；恒星自转轴也呈现类似形式但振幅约为DM的60%；初始原晕自转轴关联更强，说明非线性演化削弱了关联；映射模型无需额外拟合即可复现实空与红移空间的整体趋势。

Conclusion: 当前DM晕与恒星自转轴的空间关联性仍携带初始潮汐场信息，其总体行为可由LTTT框架结合分离映射合理解释，支持潮汐扭矩机制在结构形成中的持续作用。

Abstract: We investigate correlations of halo and stellar spin axes in the TNG300 volume of the IllustrisTNG simulations and compare them with a model based on linear tidal torque theory (LTTT). Dark matter (DM) halo spin axes at $z = 0$ exhibit positive correlations as a function of Lagrangian pair separation. The LTTT-based model describes their separation dependence well, with weak variation in the fitted amplitude across mass thresholds. A Lagrangian-to-Eulerian separation mapping based on the conditional distributions of initial separations at fixed present-day separation, measured directly from the simulations, allows the model to reproduce the overall trends in both real and redshift space without additional fitting. The hydrodynamic and DM-only runs generally show similar DM halo spin-axis correlations, although the DM-only run exhibits a steeper rise at small real-space separations. In the hydrodynamic run, stellar spin-axis correlations are consistent with the same Lagrangian form as their DM halo counterparts, but with approximately 60% of the DM correlation amplitudes. Positive stellar correlations persist in both real and redshift space, and the mapped model captures their separation dependence. Initial spin-axis correlations of protohalos are substantially stronger than their present-day counterparts at the same Lagrangian separations, consistent with subsequent nonlinear spin evolution weakening these correlations. These results suggest that present-day halo and stellar spin-axis correlations retain signatures of the initial tidal field and that their general behavior can be understood through LTTT-based modeling combined with pair-separation mapping.

</details>


### [143] [Small-scale priors for large-scale cosmology: the case of $b_φ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34212)
*Mathilde Pinon,Raul E. Angulo,Sergio Contreras,Azadeh Moradinezhad Dizgah*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用小尺度星系成团性与经验性星系形成模型（SHAMe）结合的方法，以数据驱动方式约束局部原初非高斯性（PNG）相关的偏置参数 b_φ，从而提升 f_NL 测量的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确确定 b_φ 对获得稳健的 f_NL 约束至关重要；但现有方法依赖模拟校准的普适关系，忽略 b_φ 的不确定性，可能引入偏差。

Method: 采用扩展的子晕丰度匹配模型（SHAMe）拟合小尺度星系成团性观测，并结合暗物质单独宇宙模拟推断 b_φ 后验分布；在 IllustrisTNG 水动力学单独宇宙模拟中验证该方法。

Result: SHAMe 在无需水动力学模拟校准下预测 b_φ 的中位误差为 10%，显著优于固定 HOD 模型（38%）和普适关系（23%）；残余误差主要来自选择阈值附近的中心星系。

Conclusion: 小尺度星系成团性结合基于子晕的经验模型可预测大部分星系丰度对局部 PNG 的响应；剩余部分反映星系形成物理，应作为理论不确定性纳入 b_φ 先验，从而实现数据驱动的偏置参数先验。

Abstract: Perturbative descriptions of galaxy clustering introduce free bias parameters that capture the complex physics of galaxy formation. Local primordial non-Gaussianity (PNG), parametrized by $f_{NL}$, induces a scale-dependent contribution to the galaxy bias that is proportional to $f_{NL} b_φ$, where $b_φ$ quantifies how galaxy formation responds to PNG. Accurately determining $b_φ$ is therefore essential for robust constraints on $f_{NL}$. Current analyses usually adopt a modified universal relation, $b_φ= 2δ_c(b_1-p)$, with $p$ calibrated to simulations. This relies on the accuracy of the simulated galaxy-formation model and ignores the uncertainty on $b_φ$, potentially biasing $f_{NL}$ constraints. We investigate whether $b_φ$ can be constrained from small-scale galaxy clustering using a flexible empirical galaxy-formation model. We fit an extended Subhalo Abundance Matching (SHAMe) model to small-scale clustering measurements and combine it with dark-matter-only separate-universe simulations to infer a posterior on $b_φ$. We validate the method using IllustrisTNG hydrodynamical separate-universe simulations. Without calibration on hydrodynamical simulations, SHAMe predicts $b_φ$ with a median error of 10%, compared with 38% for a fixed halo occupation distribution (HOD) that neglects the occupation response to $φ$, and 23% for the universality relation. The residual error is dominated by central galaxies near the selection threshold and persists when using the true stellar-mass assignment conditioned on the same subhalo properties. Small-scale clustering combined with a subhalo-based empirical model predicts most of galaxy-abundance response to local PNG. The remainder depends on galaxy-formation physics and should enter $b_φ$ priors as a theoretical uncertainty. This provides a route to data-informed priors on bias parameters.

</details>


### [144] [Scaling and Cross-Simulation Generalization of Graph Neural Networks for Cosmic-Web Classification in CAMELS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34332)
*Kalash Thakkar,Unnati Shah*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文测试了图神经网络（GNN）在不同宇宙学流体模拟间对星系宇宙网环境分类的泛化能力，发现基于邻域信息的GNN（尤其是图Transformer）显著优于多层感知机（MLP），但性能受限于示踪星系采样密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图方法可用于依据暗物质宇宙网环境对星系分类，但其在非开发用模拟中的可靠性尚不明确；星系形成模型会改变示踪星系的分布，进而影响图特征。

Method: 在CAMELS的IllustrisTNG、SIMBA和Astrid三个z=0流体模拟套件中，基于冷暗物质密度场对恒星质量大于10^9太阳质量的星系标注宇宙网环境（空洞/片/丝/结）；构建周期性Delaunay图并提取10个几何描述符；对比MLP与5种GNN（含局部图Transformer）在按实现分组评估下的学习曲线与跨模拟迁移性能。

Result: 图Transformer、GAT+和EdgeConv在外部保持测试集上平均宏F1分数分别为0.652、0.651和0.642，显著高于MLP的0.481；空洞类最难分类；性能随选星系数量增多和Delaunay边长变短而提升；考虑星系丰度和空洞分数后，与宇宙学及反馈参数的关联减弱。

Conclusion: GNN（尤其图Transformer）在跨模拟迁移中表现稳健，验证了邻域信息对宇宙网分类的价值；但示踪星系采样仍是暗物质宇宙网推断的重要瓶颈。

Abstract: Graph-based methods can classify galaxies by their dark-matter cosmic-web environments, but reliability beyond development simulations remains uncertain. Galaxy-formation models alter tracer populations and spatial distributions, potentially shifting graph features. We test how graph neural networks scale with independent training realizations and transfer across hydrodynamical simulations. Using the $z=0$ CAMELS IllustrisTNG, SIMBA and Astrid suites, we label galaxies with $M_\star>10^9\,M_\odot$ as void, sheet, filament or knot from the smoothed cold-dark-matter density field. We represent their spatial distributions as periodic Delaunay graphs with ten geometric descriptors. We compare a multilayer perceptron (MLP) and five graph neural networks (GNNs), including a local graph Transformer, under realization-grouped evaluation. Learning curves increase training data from 10 to 100 realizations, with validation and test boxes fixed. We test every source-to-target suite pairing on 100 unseen realizations per target suite. On this external holdout, the Transformer, GAT+ and EdgeConv reach mean per-realization macro-averaged F1 scores of $0.652$, $0.651$ and $0.642$, against $0.481$ for the MLP. The Transformer outperforms the MLP by 0.171, while the difference between the Transformer and GAT+ is not statistically significant. Voids remain the hardest class. Performance is higher in boxes with more selected galaxies and shorter Delaunay edges. Associations with cosmological and feedback parameters weaken after accounting for galaxy richness and void fraction. Our results benchmark the scaling and cross-simulation transfer of galaxy-graph classifiers, demonstrating the value of neighbourhood information and identifying tracer sampling as an important limitation for dark-matter web inference.

</details>


### [145] [Physical quasar pairs in DESI DR1 as tracers of protoclusters at $z\approx2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34350)
*Martin G. Richiarte,Diego G. Lambas*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI DR1数据构建了迄今为止最大的物理类星体对样本，发现投影距离≤2.5 Mpc的物理类星体对能有效示踪红移z≈2处的原星系团环境，其成团性显著高于随机配对，对应暗晕质量约1.4–2.2×10¹³ h⁻¹M⊙，与ΛCDM模型预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期SDSS发现的类星体对数量不足，难以系统研究其环境随分离尺度的变化；本文旨在利用更大样本（DESI DR1）检验不同投影分离尺度下的类星体对是否可作为原星系团示踪体。

Method: 从DESI DR1中筛选红移1.2≤z≤2.8、速度差|ΔV|≤900 km/s、投影距离r_p≤4 Mpc的类星体对；评估纯度与光纤完备率；测量类星体对成员相对于全部DESI类星体的空间成团性（两点相关函数）。

Result: 获得40626个r_p≤2.5 Mpc的高纯度物理类星体对；其成团性在r_p≤1、1–2.5、2.5–4 Mpc区间分别为全部类星体的1.69±0.14、1.78±0.09、2.01±0.06倍，而随机配对仅1.08±0.05倍；对应暗晕质量~1.4–2.2×10¹³ h⁻¹M⊙。

Conclusion: 投影距离≤2.5 Mpc的物理类星体对是z≈2原星系团的有效示踪体，结果与ΛCDM模拟（AbacusSummit）一致；未来完整DESI巡天将实现‘宇宙正午’时期原星系团的统计普查。

Abstract: Close pairs of quasars at the same redshift are expected to mark overdense regions that will collapse into galaxy clusters. Earlier work with SDSS found about 2800 such pairs, too few to follow their environment with separation. We build the largest sample of physical quasar pairs to date from DESI DR1 and test whether pairs out to Mpc separations trace protoclusters. We select pairs with rest-frame velocity difference $|ΔV|\leq900 km/s$ and projected separation $r_p\leq4$~Mpc at $1.2\leq z\leq2.8$, measure their purity and fibre completeness, and measure the clustering of pair members around all DESI quasars. We find 40\,626 physical pairs with projected separation $r_p\leq2.5$~Mpc, where their purity falls to 50\%, the effective radius of protoclusters at $z\approx2$. Pair members are $1.69\pm0.14$, $1.78\pm0.09$ and $2.01\pm0.06$ times more clustered than all quasars for projected separations $r_p\leq1$, 1--2.5 and 2.5--4~Mpc, while chance pairs show little excess ($1.08\pm0.05$). This corresponds to haloes of $\sim1.4$--$2.2\times10^{13}\,h^{-1}M_\odot$. Physical quasar pairs with projected separations up to 2.5~Mpc trace protocluster environments, at the level that AbacusSummit mocks predict in $Λ$CDM. The full DESI survey will turn them into a statistical census of protoclusters at cosmic noon.

</details>


### [146] [Galaxy cluster thermodynamic profiles beyond self-similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34405)
*E. Baraldi,G. W. Pratt,E. Pointecouteau*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用92个通过苏尼亚耶夫-泽尔多维奇效应选样的星系团，研究其热力学性质（密度、温度、压力、熵）的径向分布与红移、质量的标度关系，发现除压力在核心外近似自相似外，其余量均显著偏离自相似预期，且这种非自相似性延伸至大半径（~R500c），内在弥散在中间半径区（~0.2–0.8 R500c）最小，是降低标度关系弥散的最佳范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团形成本应是自相似和无尺度的引力塌缩过程，但其热介质（ICM）的热力学性质常偏离自相似预测，需系统刻画其径向、质量与红移依赖的偏差。

Method: 采用能同时允许红移和质量标度随半径变化的灵活框架，分析92个SZ选样星系团（质量范围0.5–20×10¹⁴ M⊙，红移0.08 < z < 1.13）的密度、温度、压力和熵的标度剖面，并联合约束其内禀弥散；还评估了质量代理偏移、质量依赖性及弥散增大对质量标度的影响。

Result: 所有热力学剖面均在核心区域最强、并延伸至~R500c处显著偏离自相似；压力在核心外近似自相似（无质量依赖），熵偏离最显著；内禀弥散在中心最高、在~0.2–0.8 R500c处最低（≈20%），该区域最利于降低标度关系弥散；质量标度形状稳定，但归一化受真实质量与代理间质量依赖性和弥散影响。

Conclusion: 星系团热力学分布呈现非自相似、质量依赖的标度行为，且该行为远超核心区域，表明外围并非纯引力演化；压力是唯一近似自相似的量，而中间半径区是优化标度关系的关键区域。

Abstract: Galaxy cluster formation is a self-similar and scale-free process of gravitational collapse. However, the thermodynamic properties of the intracluster medium often deviate from self-similar predictions. We investigate these deviations using a sample of 92 galaxy clusters selected through their Sunyaev-Zeldovich effect signal, spanning a mass range of $M_{500c}=[0.5-20] \times 10^{14}{M}_{\odot}$, and with redshifts $0.08 < z < 1.13$. Adopting a flexible framework that allows for radial variations in both redshift and mass scaling, we characterise the behaviour of scaled density, temperature, pressure, and entropy profiles, while simultaneously constraining their intrinsic scatter. We detect significant radial deviations from self-similar expectations in both redshift and mass scalings in all profiles. These deviations are strongest in the core regions, but remain measurable out to large radii ($\sim R_{500c}$), indicating that cluster outer regions are not fully described by purely gravitational evolution. We investigate the impact of an offset, a mass-dependence, and increased scatter between the mass proxy and the true mass on the radial mass scaling, finding that while its shape remains stable, its normalisation depends on the latter two effects. The pressure is the only thermodynamic quantity that exhibits approximately self-similar behaviour: outside the core it evolves self-similarly, with no dependence on mass. The entropy exhibits the strongest deviations from self-similarity as a function of radius. The intrinsic scatter peaks in the central regions and reaches a minimum at intermediate radii ($\lesssim 20 \%$ at $\sim 0.2$-$0.8 \ R_{500c}$), making this range optimal to reduce scatter in scaling relations. We conclude that the thermodynamic distributions exhibit non-self similar, mass-dependent scaling extending well beyond the core regime.

</details>


### [147] [A Genetic Algorithm Approach to Reconstructing Scalar-Tensor Lagrangians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34512)
*Mikel Artola,George Alestas,Dimitrios Efstratiou,Evangelos Achilleas Paraskevas*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的框架，利用遗传算法从观测数据中直接重构标量-张量理论的拉格朗日量，以解释DESIBAO等数据暗示的动力学暗能量穿越幽灵界限现象，并在满足多方面观测约束下获得优于ΛCDM和CPL参数化的拟合结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期DESI BAO、CMB和Ia型超新星数据暗示暗能量状态方程w可能穿越幽灵界限（w=-1），但标准最小耦合标量场无法平滑实现该行为，而标量-张量理论可自然实现；因此需发展能直接从数据重构理论结构的新方法。

Method: 采用遗传算法对非最小耦合函数F(φ)和标量势U(φ)进行符号表达式演化；对每个候选模型通过前向建模自洽计算宇宙膨胀历史、有效引力耦合及结构增长；联合DESIDR2 BAO、Pantheon+超新星（SH0ES定标）、压缩CMB距离先验及fσ8红移空间畸变数据构造联合似然函数进行评估；同时施加无屏蔽分支限制、太阳系γ参数与G变化约束、以及BBN对G演化的限制。

Result: 在64次独立运行中，最优重构模型相较ΛCDM改善Δχ² = -16.6，优于CPL参数化（Δχ² = -10.6）；优选耦合形式为F(φ)=c₁φ²+e^φ(1−c₂φ)；该模型在z≳0.5呈现幽灵行为，但在低红移仍接近w=−1。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的标量-张量理论重构是可行且有竞争力的方法，能自然解释w穿越−1的现象，同时满足多重观测约束，为超越ΛCDM的暗能量本质研究提供了新路径。

Abstract: Recent DESI baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) data, combined with cosmic microwave background (CMB) and Type Ia supernovae observations, have been interpreted as evidence for dynamical dark energy whose equation of state crosses the phantom divide $w=-1$. Such a crossing cannot be realized smoothly by a minimally coupled scalar field $\varphi$, but can arise naturally in scalar-tensor theories. We present a data-driven framework that reconstructs the scalar-tensor Lagrangian directly from observations, using a genetic algorithm to evolve the symbolic expressions for the nonminimal coupling $F(\varphi)$ and the scalar potential $U(\varphi)$. Each candidate is evaluated through a forward model that self-consistently determines the expansion history, the effective gravitational coupling, and the growth of structure. Candidates are ranked by a joint likelihood of DESI DR2 BAO, Pantheon+ supernovae calibrated with SH0ES Cepheids, compressed CMB distance priors, and redshift-space distortion measurements of $fσ_8$. We restrict to the unscreened branch of the theory and impose Solar System constraints on the post-Newtonian parameter $γ$ and on the time variation of the gravitational coupling, together with a Big Bang nucleosynthesis bound on its evolution. Across 64 independent runs, the best reconstruction improves the fit relative to $Λ$CDM by $Δχ^2=-16.6$, compared with $Δχ^2=-10.6$ for the CPL dark-energy parametrization. The preferred coupling is well described by $F(\varphi)=c_1\varphi^2+e^{\varphi}(1-c_2\varphi)$. The reconstructed theory shows phantom behavior at $z\gtrsim0.5$, but it remains close to $w=-1$ at low redshift.

</details>


### [148] [Probing Dynamical Dark Energy with Hyper Suprime-Cam Year 3 Cosmic Shear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34650)
*Shreya Mukherjee,Surhud More*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用HSC-Y3弱引力透镜数据结合BAO、SNe和BBN等多探针观测，对常数型（wCDM）和时变型（w0waCDM）暗能量状态方程进行约束，发现w0waCDM模型对动力学暗能量有2.4σ偏好，但弱透镜振幅S8在不同模型下保持稳健，表明当前动力学信号主要来自几何距离测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期BAO和Ia型超新星观测暗示暗能量状态方程可能随时间演化，而弱引力透镜可同时敏感于宇宙膨胀历史和结构增长，是检验此类扩展模型的理想工具。

Method: 基于HSC-Y3巡天的实空间宇宙剪切两点相关函数（2PCFs），联合DESI DR2 BAO、DOVEKIE超新星样本及BBN先验，对wCDM和w0waCDM模型进行参数约束，并与ΛCDM基准结果对比。

Result: wCDM中得w = -0.936^{+0.074}_{-0.067}；w0waCDM中得w0 = -0.832^{+0.071}_{-0.059}、wa = -1.23^{+0.66}_{-0.44}（2.4σ偏好动力学暗能量）；S8分别为0.774±0.031和0.772±0.031，与ΛCDM一致。

Conclusion: 弱透镜对S8的约束在扩展暗能量模型下稳健，当前动力学暗能量信号主要源于几何距离探针，为下一代Stage-IV巡天提供了关键一致性检验依据。

Abstract: Recent measurements of baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO) and Type Ia Supernovae (SNe) have revealed a preference for a time-evolving dark energy equation of state. Because such dynamical behavior alters both the cosmic expansion history and the late-time growth of structure, weak gravitational lensing measurements can be used to test these extended models. We present an extended analysis of the real-space cosmic shear two-point correlation functions (2PCFs) from the Hyper Suprime-Cam Year 3 (HSC-Y3) survey. Using over 25 million galaxies across 416 deg$^2$, divided into four tomographic redshift bins ($0.3 < z \le 1.5$), we constrain both a constant ($w$CDM) and a time-varying ($w_0w_a$CDM) dark energy equation of state. To break the geometric degeneracies inherent in cosmic shear, we combine the HSC-Y3 data with a CMB-independent suite of background distance probes: DESI DR2 BAO, the recalibrated DES five-year SN sample (DOVEKIE), and Big Bang Nucleosynthesis (BBN) priors. In the $w$CDM paradigm, our joint analysis yields $w =-0.936^{+0.074}_{-0.067}$, consistent with a cosmological constant. Within the $w_0w_a$CDM framework, we find $w_0 =-0.832^{+0.071}_{-0.059}$ and $w_a = -1.23^{+0.66}_{-0.44}$, representing a $2.4σ$ preference for dynamical dark energy. Although the unconstrained dark energy parameters reduce the cosmic shear-only $S_8$ constraints to smaller values, our joint multi-probe analysis agrees with the baseline HSC-Y3 $Λ$CDM structure growth amplitude. We obtain $S_8 = 0.774^{+0.027}_{-0.034}$ for $w$CDM and $S_8 = 0.772^{+0.031}_{-0.030}$ for $w_0w_a$CDM. Our results demonstrate that the weak lensing amplitude is robust to extended dark energy parameterizations, and that the dynamical dark energy signal is currently driven primarily by the geometric distance ladders, a critical consistency test for next-generation Stage-IV cosmological surveys.

</details>


### [149] [Signals from the early Universe: primordial features from the full-shape analysis of BOSS galaxies and eBOSS quasars in combination with Planck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34672)
*Mario Ballardini,Nicola Barbieri,Antonio Raffaelli,Chiara Moretti,Oliver H. E. Philcox,Emiliano Sefusatti,Francesco Verdiani*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用Planck PR4、BOSS和eBOSS数据联合搜寻原初振荡特征，采用有效场论与红外重求和方法分析CMB与大尺度结构功率谱，未发现统计显著的振荡信号；LSS单独约束弱于Planck，联合分析未改善CMB限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测暴胀时期可能产生的原初振荡特征，以检验早期宇宙物理模型（如非标准暴胀、多场暴胀等）。

Method: 结合Planck PR4 CMB数据与BOSS/eBOSS星系/类星体大尺度结构数据，对功率谱多极进行全形状分析，使用单圈有效场论并分别对各振荡成分施加红外重求和；考虑四种傅里叶空间振荡模板（线性/对数振荡，振幅为常数/局域化/幂律衰减），分别及联合拟合CMB与LSS数据。

Result: 未发现统计显著的原初振荡特征：LSS最佳拟合仅改善χ²约−6至−10，全局显著性≤1.6σ（已计数先验）；CMB中最强信号降至∼2.6σ；LSS对振幅的限制比Planck弱1.4–3倍；联合分析未改变Planck约束，因LSS不支持CMB偏好的频率。

Conclusion: 当前CMB与LSS数据均不支持存在显著原初振荡特征；未来更精确的LSS数据或可提升联合探测灵敏度。

Abstract: We search for primordial oscillatory features using cosmic microwave background (CMB) and large-scale structure (LSS) data, combining \textit{Planck} Public Release 4 (PR4) with galaxy clustering from the Baryon Oscillation Spectroscopic Survey (BOSS) and quasars from the extended Baryon Oscillation Spectroscopic Survey (eBOSS).
  For the LSS data we perform a full-shape analysis of the power spectrum multipoles with the one-loop effective field theory of large-scale structure, applying infrared resummation separately to each oscillatory component. We consider four templates: linearly and logarithmically oscillating features in Fourier space, with constant, localised, or power-law-decaying amplitudes, and fit them to the CMB and LSS data separately and jointly.
  We find no statistically significant evidence for primordial features in either dataset, individually or in combination. The best-fitting LSS features improve the fit by $Δχ^2\simeq-6$ to $-10$, which corresponds to global significances of at most $1.6\,σ$ once the look-elsewhere effect is accounted for. In the CMB alone, the most significant feature is reduced to $\sim2.6\,σ$. Over the full frequency range, the LSS bounds on the feature amplitudes are weaker than those from \textit{Planck} by factors of $1.4$--$3$. Combining \textit{Planck} with \textit{BOSS+eBOSS} leaves the CMB constraints essentially unchanged, because the LSS data do not favour the frequencies preferred by the CMB.

</details>
